### [1] [1-Bounded Entropy for $C^*$-Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15409)
*Connor MacMahon*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文针对Voiculescu提出的问题，为C*-代数定义了两种1-有界熵的概念（h和h^top），证明了h^top不超过h，引入了计算h的变分原理，并通过计算h得出结构结论（C*-素性、交叉积不可分解性、自由不可分解性），最后将熵概念推广到算子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Voiculescu提出的关于C*-代数熵概念的问题，为C*-代数建立合适的1-有界熵理论，并利用熵作为工具来推导代数结构性质。

Method: 定义了两种1-有界熵：h涉及对所有迹的迹完备化，h^top依赖于算子范数微态。通过变分原理计算h，并将这些概念推广到算子系统，证明可在基上计算。

Result: 证明了h^top(A) ≤ h(A)对所有C*-代数成立；计算了多种例子中的h值，并据此证明了C*-素性、交叉积不可分解性和自由不可分解性等结构结论；在算子系统中两种熵均可基于基计算。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了C*-代数上两种1-有界熵的完整理论框架，不仅回应了Voiculescu的问题，还展示了熵作为结构分析工具的强大效力，并将其推广到更一般的算子系统框架中。

Abstract: Towards a question of Voiculescu, two notions of $1$-bounded entropy, $h$ and $h^\text{top}$, are defined for $C^*$-algebras. The quantity $h$ involves the tracial completion with respect to all traces while the quantity $h^\text{top}$ depends on operator norm microstates. It is demonstrated that $h^\text{top}(\mathscr{A})$ does not exceed $h(\mathscr{A})$ for all $C^*$-algebras $\mathscr{A}$. Moreover, a variational principle enabling the computation of $h$ is introduced. The quantity $h$ is computed in various examples, and the computation is used to justify structural conclusions such as $C^*$-primeness, crossed product indecomposability, and free indecomposability. These notions of entropy are generalized to operator systems, where it is demonstrated that both may be computed on a basis.

</details>


### [2] [Operator Systems in Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16395)
*Markus Dannemüller,Tim Netzer*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文针对无限维序单位空间对偶理论中缺乏序单位的核心困难，引入了一种算子系统的相对对偶概念：两个系统在每一矩阵层级上通过底层向量空间的配对诱导锥配对时即处于对偶关系。证明了可分B(H)、具有忠实迹的C*-代数等算子系统存在对偶，而有些算子系统则不具备对偶性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限维序单位空间的通常对偶不一定携带序单位，这是算子系统对偶理论困难的主要原因。作者并未试图计算一个典范的对偶对象，而是引入了一个相对性的对偶概念来绕开这一根本障碍。

Method: 定义了算子系统的相对对偶概念，即当两个系统底层向量空间的配对在每个矩阵层级诱导锥配对时，称两系统处于对偶关系。在此基础上发展了该对偶概念的基本性质，包括子系统、商空间和适当的张量积在对偶下的封闭性。

Result: 证明了有限维算子系统以及可分的B(H)和具有忠实迹的C*-代数都存在对偶，而某些算子系统则不具有对偶。还证明了有限层级极大性与有限层级极小性互相对偶，并建立了到矩阵代数ℓ∞积的弱连续和弱*闭实现。

Conclusion: 该相对对偶理论为无限维算子系统的对偶性研究提供了一个新的有效框架，绕过了经典对偶理论的瓶颈，并揭示了有限层级极大性与极小性之间的对偶关系。

Abstract: The usual dual of an infinite-dimensional order-unit space need not carry an order unit, and this is the main reason why duality for operator systems is notoriously hard. Rather than trying to compute a canonical dual object, we introduce a relative notion of duality for operator systems: Two systems are in duality when a pairing of their underlying vector spaces induces conic pairings at every matrix level. We develop basic properties of this notion, showing that beyond finite-dimensional operator systems, also separable $B(H)$ and $C^*$-algebras with a faithful trace admit duals, while some other operator systems do not. We prove that subsystems, quotients and suitable tensor products of systems with duals again admit duals. As consequences, we show that finite-level maximality is dual to finite-level minimality, and we establish weakly continuous and weak$^*$ closed realizations into $\ell^\infty$-products of matrix algebras.

</details>


### [3] [Ground states on the \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebras of Fell bundles over étale groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16588)
*Md Amir Hossain*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Fell丛C*-代数的实动力学σ^c的基态空间。作者建立了σ^c基态空间与边界群胚G(Z)上的C*-代数的态空间之间的仿射同胚，其中Z是1-上闭链c的边界集。特别地，C*(G;A)存在σ^c基态当且仅当Z非空。此外，作者探讨了基态与KMS_∞态的关系，并在上闭链局部常数时给出了边界C*-代数的结晶化解释，最后应用于Deaconu--Renault群胚的扭曲C*-代数和高阶图C*-代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Fell丛C*-代数上由实值1-上闭链诱导的实动力学的基态，是C*-代数动力系统理论中的核心问题之一。基态空间的结构刻画有助于深入理解C*-代数的表示理论和群胚结构的边界行为，但目前对Fell丛框架下基态的完整描述仍然缺乏系统性的研究。

Method: 本文方法包括：（1）利用上闭链c的边界集Z和边界群胚G(Z)建立基态与边界C*-代数态空间之间的仿射同胚；（2）通过分析基态与KMS_∞态之间关系揭示两者在渐近行为上的联系；（3）对局部常数上闭链情形给出边界C*-代数的结晶化诠释；（4）将一般理论应用于Deaconu--Renault群胚和高阶图C*-代数等具体例类以得到显式基态描述。

Result: 建立了C*(G;A)的σ^c基态空间与C*(G(Z);A|_{G(Z)})的态空间之间的仿射同胚；证明了基态存在性与边界集非空的等价性；阐明了基态与KMS_∞态的关联；对Deaconu--Renault群胚与高阶图C*-代数给出了基态的显式刻画。

Conclusion: 本文在Fell丛C*-代数框架下完整刻画了由1-上闭链诱导的实动力学的基态空间，揭示了其与边界群胚C*-代数态空间的本质联系，并将结果应用于多个重要例类，为该方向后续研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Let \(p\colon \mathcal{A}\to G\) be a Fell bundle over a locally compact Hausdorff second countable étale groupoid \(G\) and let \(\mathrm{C}^*(G; \mathcal{A})\) be the associated Fell bundle \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebra. Suppose \(σ^c\) is the real dynamics on \(\mathrm{C}^*(G;\mathcal{A})\) induced by a real-valued \(1\)-cocycle \(c\). We establish an affine homeomorphism between the \(σ^c\)-ground state space of \(\mathrm{C}^*(G;\mathcal{A})\) and the state space of \(\mathrm{C}^*(G(Z); \mathcal{A}|_{G(Z)})\), where \(Z\) is the boundary set of \(c\) and \(G(Z)\) is the boundary groupoid. In particular, \(\mathrm{C}^*(G; \mathcal{A})\) admits \(σ^c\)-ground states if and only if \(Z\neq \emptyset\). We further investigate the relation between ground states and KMS\(_{\infty}\) states and give a crystallization interpretation of the boundary \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebra \(\mathrm{C}^*(G(Z); \mathcal{A}|_{G(Z)})\) when the cocycle is locally constant. Finally, we apply our results to several classes of examples, including twisted \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebras of Deaconu--Renault groupoids and twisted higher-rank graph \(\mathrm{C}^*\)-algebras, obtaining explicit descriptions of their ground states.

</details>


### [4] [Quantum channels on duals of von Neumann algebras in the Schrödinger picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15116)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文在薛定谔图景下，将量子信道定义为一般冯·诺依曼代数对偶上的完全正映射，并利用拓扑群表示的Pettis积分来构造此类信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子信道理论要么局限于有限维态空间，要么在海森堡图景下作为C*-代数上的完全正映射进行研究，缺乏在薛定谔图景下针对一般冯·诺依曼代数对偶空间的系统研究。

Method: 将量子信道推广为一般冯·诺依曼代数对偶上的完全正映射（薛定谔图景），并利用拓扑群表示构造Pettis积分，以此构建满足完全正性的信道。

Result: 在一般冯·诺依曼代数对偶空间中建立了量子信道的构造框架，通过拓扑群表示的Pettis积分实现了信道的显式构造。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子信道理论从有限维和C*-代数框架扩展到一般冯·诺依曼代数的对偶空间，为更广义的量子信息理论提供了数学基础。

Abstract: The theory of quantum channels is traditionally studied either on finite-dimensional state spaces or within the Heisenberg picture as completely positive maps on C^*-algebras. In this paper, we consider quantum channels as completely positive maps on the duals of general von Neumann algebras in the Schrodinger picture. We investigate the construction of such channels through Pettis integrals using representations of topological groups.

</details>

### [5] [Entrywise Loewner Preservers on Min and Max Matrix Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15125)
*Wei Xie*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了由实序列生成的Min矩阵和Max矩阵上的逐项函数映射，给出了保持正半定性、全非负性、Loewner序和Loewner凸性的精确刻画。主要结果显示：逐项保持Loewner序等价于f单调不减且凸；在Min和Max锥上要求Loewner凸性不等式对所有任意对成立会强制f为仿射函数，而限制在Loewner可比较对上则自动推出f∈C¹([0,∞))，且等价于f与f'均为凸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Min矩阵A_min(x)=(x_min(i,j))和Max矩阵A_max(x)=(x_max(i,j))是两类具有特殊结构的矩阵族。理解哪些逐项函数映射能保持这些矩阵的重要性质（如正半定性、全非负性、Loewner序等），在矩阵分析、插值理论和优化领域有重要意义。作者旨在利用这两类矩阵的经典锥参数化，给出这些保持性质的完全特征描述，特别是Loewner序和凸性结构。

Method: 利用Min和Max矩阵的经典锥参数化方法。作者将性质保持问题转化为对函数f的性质刻画问题，不假设连续性或可微性，通过锥结构和Loewner序的内在几何性质推导出函数条件。对于Loewner凸性，区分了两种情况：对任意对的凸性不等式（过于刚性）和对Loewner可比较对的凸性不等式（标准约定）。

Result: (1) 逐项Loewner序保持等价于f非递减且凸；(2) 在Min和Max锥上，Loewner凸性对所有任意对成立则f必须仿射；(3) 对Loewner可比较对的凸性，自动推出f∈C¹([0,∞))，且等价于f和f'均为凸（Loewner凹性类似）；(4) 所有情形在二维中即可被检测；(5) 正半定型保持等价于f非负且非递减，同样条件刻画全非负性保持；(6) 确定了幂函数的保持范围、Loewner序自同构，并描述了严格Min/Max类与逆M矩阵及振动矩阵的联系。

Conclusion: 本文对Min和Max矩阵族上的逐项映射给出了紧凑且完全的刻画。核心发现是Loewner结构在这些锥上极其刚性：无正则性假设下Loewner序保持即等价于凸性；而Loewner凸性在通常约定下自动隐含C¹光滑性。所有性质均在二维情形即可完全检测，说明这些矩阵族的结构维度本质上是二维的。严格类的刻画还与逆M矩阵和振动矩阵建立了新的联系。

Abstract: Let \[ A_{\min}(x)=\bigl(x_{\min(i,j)}\bigr)_{i,j=1}^n, \qquad A_{\max}(x)=\bigl(x_{\max(i,j)}\bigr)_{i,j=1}^n \] be the Min and Max matrices generated by a real sequence \(x=(x_1,\ldots,x_n)\). Using their classical cone parametrizations, we give exact characterizations of entrywise maps preserving positive semidefiniteness, total nonnegativity, Loewner order, and Loewner convexity.
  Our main results concern the Loewner structure.Without assuming continuity or differentiability, entrywise Loewner-order preservation is equivalent to \(f\) being nondecreasing and convex. On the Min and Max cones, requiring the Loewner-convexity inequality on arbitrary pairs is rigid and forces \(f\) to be affine. Under the standard convention of restricting the inequality to Loewner-comparable pairs, the condition automatically forces \(f\in C^1([0,\infty))\) and is equivalent to convexity of both \(f\) and \(f'\). The analogous statement holds for Loewner concavity. In each case, the condition is already detected in dimension two.
  We also show that \(f:[0,\infty)\to\mathbb R\) preserves positive semidefiniteness entrywise on all positive semidefinite Min or Max matrices if and only if \(f\) is nonnegative and nondecreasing; the same condition characterizes total-nonnegativity preservation. Finally, we determine the power-function ranges and Loewner-order automorphisms, characterize the entrywise preservers of the strict Min and Max classes, and relate these strict cones to inverse \(M\)-matrices and oscillatory matrices.

</details>


### [6] [A Continuous digit projector from binary representations of numbers onto $A_2$-representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15158)
*O. O. Nikorak,S. P. Ratushniak*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个连续函数f，作为经典二进制表示数字投影器到A₂-连分数表示数字的类比物（零冗余情形），证明了其良定义性、连续性和单调性，并通过二进制表示的正常性质与Lebesgue定理确立了该函数的奇异性；同时建立了该函数与右移算子及连分数表示逆变换器之间的关系，并由此证明了逆变换器的奇异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A₂-连分数表示的数字投影器是不连续函数（因其表示在拓扑上不等价于经典二进制表示），本文旨在构造一个连续的类比函数来替代不连续的数字投影器，并研究其性质（特别是奇异性）。

Method: 构造形如f(Δ²_{α₁α₂...α_{2n-1}α_{2n}...})=Δ^{A₂}_{(1/2)^{1-α₁}(1/2)^{α₂}...(1/2)^{1-α_{2n-1}}(1/2)^{α_{2n}}...}的连续函数，利用二进制表示的正常性质、Lebesgue定理（连续单调函数几乎处处有限导数存在）来证明奇异性，并通过建立与右移算子及逆变换器的关系进一步推广结论。

Result: 证明了函数f是良定义、连续且单调的；借助Lebesgue定理确立了f的奇异性（即导数几乎处处为零或不存在有限导数）；并通过f与右移算子、逆变换器之间的关系证明了逆变换器的奇异性。

Conclusion: 该连续函数f为A₂-连分数表示提供了类比二进制数字投影器的连续替代物，其奇异性揭示了A₂-连分数表示的深层测度论性质，且这一性质可传递到逆变换器上。

Abstract: As is known, the $A_2$-continued representation of numbers is not topologically equivalent to the classical binary representation; therefore, the digit projector of such representations is a discontinuous function. In this paper, we introduce a continuous function that serves as an analogue of the digit projector of the classical binary representation of numbers into the digits of the $A_2$-continued representation with zero redundancy, namely a function of the form \[f(Δ^2_{α_1α_2...α_{2n-1}α_{2n}...})= Δ^{A_2}_{(\frac{1}{2})^{1-α_1}(\frac{1}{2})^{α_2}... (\frac{1}{2})^{1-α_{2n-1}}(\frac{1}{2})^{α_{2n}}...}, α_n\in \{0,1\}.\] It is proved that the function $f$ is well-defined, continuous, and monotone. Using the normal properties of numbers with respect to their binary representation and Lebesgue's theorem asserting the existence of a finite derivative for a continuous monotone function almost everywhere, the singularity of the function $f$ is established.
  The paper also establishes a relationship between the considered function, the right-shift operator on digits, and the inversor of the continued representation of numbers. This relationship is then used to establish the singularity of the inversor.

</details>


### [7] [Commutator estimates for functions of noncommuting self-adjoint operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16731)
*Vladimir Peller*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究两个自伴算子A、B的二元函数演算φ↦φ(A,B)的性质，其交换子[A,B]属于Schatten-von Neumann类S_p。在p≤2时，该演算在Besov类B_{∞,1}^1(R²)上满足交换子Lipschitz估计，并且模S_p是可乘的；而在p>2时不存在交换子Lipschitz估计，算子范数意义下也不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自伴算子二元函数演算在交换子属于Schatten类条件下的Lipschitz性质和代数结构的可乘性问题，将经典的交换子Lipschitz估计推广到二元情形。

Method: 利用Besov类B_{∞,1}^1(R²)作为函数空间，结合Schatten-von Neumann类S_p对交换子阶次p进行分类讨论（p≤2与p>2），建立相应的交换子Lipschitz估计并检验演算的可乘性。

Result: (1) 当p≤2时，该演算在Besov类B_{∞,1}^1(R²)上具有交换子Lipschitz估计，且模S_p具有可乘性；(2) 当p>2时，交换子Lipschitz估计不成立；(3) 在算子范数（p=∞）意义下同样不存在交换子Lipschitz估计。

Conclusion: 二元函数演算的交换子Lipschitz性质存在临界指标p=2：仅在p≤2时成立，p>2及算子范数情形均失效，这揭示了Schatten类条件对该演算正则性的本质界限。

Abstract: We study properties of the calculus $\varphi\mapsto\varphi(A,B)$ for self-adjoint operators with commutator $[A,B]$ in the Schaten--von Neumann class $\boldsymbol{S}_p$. It turns out that this calculus defined on the Besov class $B_{\infty,1}^1({\Bbb R}^2)$ in the case $p\le2$ admits a commutator Lipschitz estimate and is multiplicative modulo $\boldsymbol{S}_p$. On the other hand in the case $p>2$ there is no commutator Lipschitz estimate. There is no commutator Lipschitz estimate in the operator norm as well.

</details>


### [8] [Operator Inequalities and Several Characterizations of the $λ$-Mean Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15180)
*Bikram Das,Goutam Biswas,Chandal Nahak*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文推广了Buzano型不等式，为AXB型算子提供了新的数值半径界；针对λ-均值变换M_λ(T)，给出了反例证明r_σ(M_λ(T)) ≤ r_σ(T)在一般情形下不成立，并研究了其与τ_ω、共轭性质、张量积及范数保持条件的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展已有的数值半径不等式结果（特别是Sababheh等人的工作），并深入探究λ-均值变换的谱半径、数值半径行为及其边界条件，填补该领域一般情形的空白。

Method: 通过推广Buzano型不等式来建立AXB型算子的数值半径上界；构造反例证明谱半径不等式失效；引入新的σ-类刻画共轭性质成立的充要条件；分析张量积的变换特性；精化Zamani不等式；统一算子模量界；利用范数保持条件与||T²||=||T||²的等价性推导2×2分块算子矩阵的范数估计。

Result: 成功推广了Buzano型不等式；证明r_σ(M_λ(T)) ≤ r_σ(T)一般不成立，(r_ω(M_λ(T)))^n与r_ω(T^n)对n≥2通常不可比较；给出M_λ(T*)=(M_λ(T))*成立的充要条件（T属于新σ-类）；精化Zamani不等式并统一模量界；证明范数保持条件等价于||T²||=||T||²，并提供2×2分块矩阵的精确范数估计。

Abstract: For an m-homogeneous polynomial on C^n, let D_{m,n} denote the optimal constant in the complex polynomial Bohnenblust--Hille inequality, and set D_m = sup_n D_{m,n}. Our principal result gives a quasipolynomial upper bound for the dimension-free constants:
  limsup_{m->infinity} log D_m / (log m)^2 <= 5 / [8 log(4/(1+sqrt(5)))].
  Thus the previously known exp(O(sqrt(m log m))) bound can be replaced by exp(O((log m)^2)). The proof uses a fixed-ratio degree reduction. A phase-preserving splitting retains the exact ancestry of every coefficient, and a fractional two-block estimate controls the two resulting degree scales. This gives a recurrence between macroscopically separated degrees whose iteration produces the quadratic logarithm above.

  liminf_{m->infinity} D_m > 1.27.

</details>

Abstract: We prove a sharp lower bound for the first nonzero Neumann eigenvalue of geodesic triangles of given diameter in two-dimensional space forms. ### [19] [The Existence of Non-Equivariant Gromov Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14688)
*Grigoriy Yakovlev*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究平坦环面是否等距且极小平嵌入到球面时，其平移群是否必然延伸到环境球面的等距群。通过构造非等变的嵌入Gromov环面，证明了对于n≥3维，回答是否定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题由Robert Bryant在n=2时给出肯定回答，而Ying Lu等人证明了n≥3时浸入情形为否定，但嵌入情形仍然悬而未决。该问题与Gromov和Petrunin关于最优曲率界的工作密切相关，解决嵌入情形是该领域的重要开放问题。

Method: 首先证明任何Gromov环面都是内蕴平坦的，位于某个球面内且在其中是极小的；然后建立定义这些环面的充要条件；最后构造一个非等变的嵌入Gromov环面。

Result: 对于n≥3维，构造出了一个非等变的嵌入Gromov环面，为上述问题给出了决定性的否定回答。该构造也验证了Gromov环面满足Petrunin给出的最优法曲率下界并达到该界。

Conclusion: 对于n≥3，平坦环面等距极小平嵌入球面时，其平移群不必延伸到环境球面的等距群，推翻了此前对嵌入情形的猜测，完善了该问题的完整回答。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the following question: if a flat torus $\mathbb{T}^n$ is isometrically and minimally embedded into a sphere $\mathbb{S}^N$, must its translation group extend to the isometry group of the ambient sphere?
  As shown by Robert Bryant, for $n=2$ the answer is positive. Furthermore, while Ying Lu, Peng Wang, and Zhenxiao Xie recently demonstrated that the answer is negative for immersions when $n \geq 3$, the question for embeddings remained open.
  This problem is deeply tied to the work of Mikhail Gromov and Anton Petrunin concerning optimal curvature bounds. Petrunin proved that any immersion of a torus into a unit ball must have a maximum normal curvature of at least $\sqrt{\frac{3n}{n+2}}$. This bound is attained, for example, by families of tori constructed by Gromov. We call the tori that attain this optimal bound "Gromov tori".
  In this work, we first demonstrate that any Gromov torus is intrinsically flat, lies within a sphere, and is minimal inside it. We then establish the necessary and sufficient conditions for defining these tori. Finally, we present our main result: for dimensions $n \ge 3$, there exists a non-equivariant embedded Gromov torus, which provides a definitive negative answer to the question above.

</details>

Abstract: In the previous paper \cite{Jin26}, the author introduced the notions of an adapted current $T$ and an adapted Hermitian-Einstein metric to establish the Kobayashi-Hitchin correspondence for a nef and big class $α$. As a continuation of the previous work, this paper studies the solvability and the convergence of the Yang-Mills flow for a nef and big class $α$ on a holomorphic vector bundle $E$ over a compact Kähler manifold $X$. In particular, we show that the limit of the Yang-Mills flow at infinity is determined by the holomorphic structure of $E$ and the nef and big class $α$. More precisely, if we fix an integrable unitary connection $A_0$ on $E$, we show that the $T$-Yang-Mills flow on $E$ with initial condition $A_0$ is solvable for all time and it converges to a $T$-Yang-Mills connection $A_{\infty}$ in the sense of Uhlenbeck limit. Furthermore, we also show that, on the ample locus of $α$, $A_{\infty}$ is complex-gauge equivalent to the direct sum of the Chern connections of the $T$-adapted Hermitian-Einstein metrics on the factors of the graded sheaf associated with the $α^{n-1}$-Harder-Narasimhan-Seshadri filtration of $E$.

### [24] [Geometric turbulence: a geodesic-regression crisis indicator for equity covariance dynamics, with evidence from African markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15205)
*E. B. Camara,Y. U. Gaba,I. G. M. Nsiloulou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究在对称正定（SPD）流形上对资产篮子协方差矩阵进行测地线回归，使用log-Euclidean和仿射不变度量，并提出拟合测地线的速度范数作为几何感知的波动率指标。在四个股市篮子数据上验证，该指标无需校准即可在2008金融危机、2020疫情、2022加息周期和2019埃及政治经济紧张等压力事件上清晰产生峰值。log-Euclidean回归在崩溃窗口中的测地线均方误差比欧氏OLS小150-300倍。简单的速度感知去风险策略以1.4个百分点年化收益为代价将最大回撤降低2.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵是定义在弯曲流形（SPD锥）上的对称正定对象，而非平坦向量空间。标准线性回归在向量化协方差元素上操作，忽略了这一几何结构，在市场压力期可能产生非正定（非SPD）的拟合结果，导致预测失效。作者旨在提出一种几何感知的回归方法及相应的市场波动指标。

Method: 在SPD(n)锥上采用log-Euclidean和仿射不变两种度量进行测地线回归，以滚动样本协方差矩阵作为数据。将拟合测地线的速度作为波动率指标（速度范数）。数据覆盖四个股票篮子：S&P 10股历史子篮子（2006-2024）、S&P 100 mega-cap篮子、JSE前十大股票、EGX前十大交易股票。采用hold-out崩溃窗口评估，并测试了基于速度的去风险交易策略。

Result: 指标在未校准的情况下对已知压力事件产生清晰峰值。在崩溃窗口的hold-out评估中，log-Euclidean回归的测地线均方误差比欧氏OLS小150至300倍（因欧氏OLS产生非SPD预测，在内蕴度量下表现爆炸式恶化）。速度感知去风险策略将最大回撤降低2.5个百分点，代价为年化收益减少1.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 测地线回归在协方差矩阵建模中显著优于忽视几何结构的欧氏方法，且拟合测地线的速度范数是一种无需校准的、可靠的波动率指标。该指标在压力信号检测上可靠，但转化为实际交易策略时效果存在损耗，说明其适合作为缓慢移动的市场压力监测工具而非精细化交易信号。

Abstract: The covariance matrix of a basket of assets is a symmetric positive-definite object that evolves on a curved manifold, not on a flat vector space. Standard linear regression on its vectorised entries ignores that geometry and, in periods of market stress, can return fits that fail to be positive definite. We study geodesic regression on the symmetric positive-definite cone $\SPD(n)$ under the log-Euclidean and affine-invariant metrics, with the rolling sample covariance of a basket of log-returns as data. The fitted geodesic's velocity norm is proposed as a geometry-aware turbulence indicator. On daily data for four equity baskets- a ten-stock historical S\&P sub-basket (2006-2024), a current S\&P~100 mega-cap basket, the top ten listings of the Johannesburg Stock Exchange (JSE), and the ten most-traded Egyptian Exchange (EGX) tickers the indicator peaks cleanly on known stress episodes (the 2008 global financial crisis, the 2020 COVID crash, the 2022 Fed rate-hike cycle, and the October-November 2019 Egyptian political-economic tension episode) without any calibration. On hold-out evaluation in crash windows, log-Euclidean regression achieves 150 to 300 times smaller geodesic mean squared error than Euclidean ordinary least squares, because the latter produces non-SPD forecasts that blow up under the intrinsic metric. A naive velocity-aware de-risking strategy reduces maximum drawdown by 2.5 percentage points at the cost of 1.4 percentage points of annual return, consistent with a slow-moving indicator that signals stress reliably but translates imperfectly into tactical trading. All data, code, and experiments are reproducible from the companion repository.

</details>

Abstract: We characterize all symmetric, embedded surfaces of revolution with constant mean curvature that meet the unit sphere orthogonally. Abstract: We establish optimal Casorati inequalities for pointwise slant Riemannian submersions.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过边界定义函数ρ_i和非负权重α_i构造两类度量g_Π和g_Σ，利用活动指标集I(p)、权重和A(p)及最大权重M(p)来分类边界点的可达性。通过分析法向方向在完备化中的坍缩行为，给出完备化的拓扑描述，并在可访问开面上计算局部边界度量的雪芬指数与Hausdorff维数。

Abstract: We study metric completions of conformally singular Riemannian metrics on the interior of a compact manifold with corners. Given boundary-defining functions \(ρ_1,\ldots,ρ_N\) and nonnegative weights \(α_1,\ldots,α_N\), we compare the product metric \(g_Π=(\prod_iρ_i^{-α_i})g_0\) with the sum metric \(g_Σ=(\sum_iρ_i^{-α_i})g_0\). For a boundary point \(p\), let \(I(p)\) be its active index set, \(A(p)=\sum_{i\in I(p)}α_i\), and \(M(p)=\max_{i\in I(p)}α_i\). Abstract: We present a systematic study on the role of invariant connections in geometric mechanics. We first develop a comprehensive theory of reduction under left- and right-invariant connections on Lie groups, showing that the Euler--Poincaré and Lie--Poisson equations are independent of the connection. We then introduce a novel connection-dependent variational principle, where the Lagrangian depends on the velocity parallel-transported back to the initial point of the curve. For Cartan--Schouten connections, this leads to an integro-differential Euler--Poincaré equation that exhibits two distinct sources of nonlocality in time: a path-dependent term encoded in the parallel transport and a future-dependent term arising from a curvature integral. We reformulate this equation as a two-point boundary value problem and present a two-level numerical scheme. The general theory is illustrated on the Heisenberg group, where the equations simplify due to nilpotency, and on the rotation group, where the full integro-differential structure is retained.

Abstract: Atiyah's Minkowski-space version of the configuration-of-points construction assigns to an admissible marked configuration of $n$ worldlines a collection of $n$ binary forms of degree $n-1$, whose roots are the ordered retarded celestial directions. He conjectured that these forms are always linearly independent.

At this parameter, all six ordered celestial roots are distinct and the coefficient matrix has rank exactly two. A null-translation construction then multiplies the first three forms by a common factor and produces counterexamples for every $n>3$. Result: 证明了h^p(R^n)的成员资格与无条件wavelet展开、三种wavelet平方函数可积性、局部原子分解及Goldberg型分裂的等价性；建立了t_p空间的原子理论（首次在p<1的拟Banach范围）；确定了Goldberg型分裂成立的精确阈值（Hölder总阶数n_p、临界衰减|x|^{-n/p}）；获得mollified原子和分子的界、系数大小条件、拟范数等价性，以及wavelet原子分解在L^2中的无条件收敛，有限截断的误差为精确Parseval恒等式。

Abstract: We extend the author's wavelet and tent space characterization of the local Hardy space $h^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$ to the full range $0 < p \le 1$. Building on Meyer's program for $H^1(\mathbb{R}^n)$ and its dyadic tent space, we introduce a local dyadic tent space $t_p$ of coefficient pairs, a father layer on the unit lattice and a mother layer on the small dyadic cubes, develop its atomic theory, and prove that membership in $h^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is equivalent to seven conditions: the unconditionality of the inhomogeneous wavelet expansion in $L^p$, the $L^p$-integrability of three wavelet square functions, atomic decompositions into local atoms adapted to cubes or balls, and a Goldberg-type splitting $f - \varphi * f \in H^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$, $\varphi * f \in L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$, tested against an admissible class of functions. The space $t_p$ and its atomic theory appear here for the first time in the quasi-Banach range $p < 1$. Over pairs of a modulus of continuity grading the regularity and a radial decreasing majorant grading the decay, we prove that the splitting holds exactly when the modulus is dominated by the Hölder modulus of exponent $n_p - \lceil n_p \rceil + 1$, $n_p := n(1/p - 1)$, and the majorant satisfies an $\ell^p$-Dini summability over the scales, iterated once more at integer $n_p$. The admissible class is thus maximal for the Goldberg-type splitting: it extends Goldberg's Schwartz class to Hölder total order exactly $n_p$ with majorants down to the critical decay $|x|^{-n/p}$, where a new molecular theory governs the embedding into $H^p$, with explicit counterexamples on each axis. Byproducts include bounds for mollified atoms and molecules, coefficient size conditions, quasi-norm equivalences, and the unconditional $L^2$-convergence of the wavelet-built atomic decomposition, with the error of every finite section an exact Parseval identity.

</details>

Abstract: We study the full three-dimensional dynamics of the $L^2$-critical Zakharov-Kuznetsov equation with the fractional nonlinearity $|u|^{4/3}u$, equivalently $u^{7/3}$ for real-valued functions. This equation is a higher-dimensional extension of the generalized Korteweg-de Vries equation. In the critical setting solutions to this 3D ZK equation may blow up in finite time or exhibit global time dynamics. The novelties of this work is to treat a non-integer power and to study the dynamics of solutions in a higher dimension.
  We first review the finite time blow-up in 2D critical ZK, then do a formal analysis of the slightly mass-supercritical blow-up dynamics, deriving the corrections to the blow-up rate and profile for the critical ZK equation in any dimension. We then perform a computational study of solutions, utilizing full 3D numerical simulations. In particular, we use a Fourier pseudospectral discretization and an integrating factor fourth-order Runge-Kutta method on a full three-dimensional grid. A multi-GPU implementation makes it possible to follow blow-up solutions in a full 3D setting. We examine perturbations of the ground state, Gaussian data, and nonsymmetric two-bump configurations. The computations show dispersive and concentrating regimes, in both cases with radiation emitted in a conic-type region opposite to the direction of propagation and convergence of the concentrating core toward a rescaled ground-state profile. The two-bump experiments also demonstrate that total mass alone does not determine the blow-up dynamics. We discuss the numerical evidence for the predicted blow-up rate and identify the pre-asymptotic and resolution limitations that remain near the blow-up time.

</details>

Abstract: This work investigates the class of SIR/SEIR models and their extension to an ODE-based SVEIR system, whose parameters are calibrated and validated using real influenza outbreak data. The theoretical part establishes existence and uniqueness of solutions for the SVEIR model via the Picard-Lindelof theorem and analyzes invariance of the admissible region. To describe spatiotemporal infection spread in an urban setting, a diffusion-chemotaxis extension of the SIR framework is formulated as a PDE system; an additional term is introduced to represent fear/avoidance effects in the system dynamics. A numerical workflow is proposed, including grid discretization, Neumann boundary conditions enforced by mirror extension, a semi-implicit IMEX time-stepping scheme, and sparse linear solves; computational correctness is monitored, in particular, by checking conservation of the total population.

Under certain assumptions on $p$ and $q$, we also prove finite-time extinction results for solutions when the graph satisfies an \textit{isoperimetric inequality}. In particular, on Cayley graphs with polynomial volume growth, we establish the optimal large-time decay rate of the $\ell^\infty$-norm for nonnegative finite-mass solutions when $q(p-1)>1$, and demonstrate a sharp dichotomy regarding the finite-time extinction of exhaustion solutions.

Abstract: We study fractional-Sobolev Tikhonov regularization for linear inverse problems on a bounded Lipschitz domain. The regularization penalty is generated by the restricted Dirichlet fractional Laplacian, and the associated variational problem is shown to admit a unique minimizer that depends Lipschitz continuously on the data. Identifying the positive self-adjoint operator $$A_s=I+(-Δ)^s,\, D(A_s^{1/2})=H_0^s(Ω),$$ we transform the problem isometrically into a classical Hilbert-space Tikhonov problem with observation operator $B=KA_s^{-1/2}$. This yields explicit mean-square error bounds and an order-optimal \emph{a priori} and \emph{a posteriori} parameter rules under Hölder-type source conditions.
  The framework is illustrated by partial observations and by the backward fractional heat equation. In the latter case, $$ B^*B=A_s^{-1}e^{-2tA_s}, $$ which permits a mode-wise description of the source condition, the singular-value decay, and the effective reconstruction bandwidth. We also study the local limit $s\to1^-$: after Bourgain--Brezis--Mironescu normalization, the fractional functionals $Γ$-converge in $L^2(Ω)$ to the classical $H_0^1$-Tikhonov functional, and the corresponding minimizers converge strongly in $L^2(Ω)$. Numerical experiments for the backward fractional heat problem illustrate the reconstruction procedure and the influence of the penalty order, and confirm the predicted mean-square convergence rate to within a few percent via Monte Carlo simulation, with Morozov's discrepancy principle attaining the same order-optimal rate a posteriori.

Conclusion: 本文表明，各向异性Calderón问题的唯一性可以通过两种互补路径获得：要么依赖拟解析性以在一般几何中实现解的延拓，要么在C^∞正则性下通过对称性或单侧序结构假设替代延拓性质。这一权衡为理解该开放问题的本质困难提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The anisotropic Calderón problem in dimensions $n\ge3$ remains open for general smooth metrics~\cite{Uhlmann2009}. We establish uniqueness results in two complementary regimes. In the first, the identity principle for quasianalytic functions propagates boundary information and yields uniqueness in general geometry, including a partial-boundary consequence; under a prescribed normal geometry, quasianalyticity is needed only in the distinguished direction. In the second, suitable symmetry or one-sided ordering assumptions lead to uniqueness at $C^\infty$ regularity with full or restricted boundary access. Taken together, the results exhibit a tradeoff among regularity, geometric structure, and boundary access: quasianalyticity supplies continuation in general geometry, while symmetry or one-sided order replaces that continuation at $C^\infty$ regularity.

The scalar theory has a rather short history, first arising in the work of G. Aronsson in the 1960s. The vectorial case is even more recent and first arose in work of the second appearing author in the early 2010s. The Calculus of Variations in $L^\infty$ is an all-important field for numerous applications, but it presents serious difficulties and requires new tools, different to those required for the classical case of integral functionals and Euler-Lagrange equations.

Abstract: We fix an integer $d\ge1$ and a symmetric positive-definite matrix $Q\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$. Let $V:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be finite, set \[ Z_μ:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^d}e^{-V(x)}\,dx\in(0,\infty), \qquad dμ(x):=Z_μ^{-1}e^{-V(x)}\,dx, \] and assume that $μ$ has finite second moment and that \[ x\longmapsto \frac12\langle Qx,x\rangle-V(x) \] is convex. Let $ν$ be a compactly supported log-concave probability measure with support $K$, and let $\nablaΦ$ be the Brenier map from $μ$ to $ν$. For $v\in\mathbb{R}^d$, define \[ w_K(v):= \sup_{y\in K}\langle y,v\rangle -
\inf_{y\in K}\langle y,v\rangle. \] We prove that \[ \partial_{vv}Φ\le 0.587 \sqrt{\langle Qv,v\rangle}\,w_K(v) \qquad(v\in\mathbb{R}^d) \] in the sense of distributions. We show that $\nablaΦ$ has an everywhere-defined globally Lipschitz representative such that \[ \operatorname{Lip}(\nablaΦ) \le 0.587 \sqrt{\|Q\|_{\mathrm{op}}}\,\operatorname{diam}(K). \] The directional Hessian estimate is affinely covariant, whereas the global Lipschitz estimate is dimension-free. The result also applies to singular or lower-dimensional targets. In particular, it removes the $\sqrt d$ loss in Kolesnikov's estimate for the Brenier map from Gaussian measure to normalised Lebesgue measure on a convex body. We also prove new bounds that depend only on the support for compactly supported semi-log-concave targets, which includes targets with bounded negative curvature.

Abstract: We prove that the global minimizer of the Ginzburg-Landau energy in the disk of radius $R$ with boundary value $ u(x)=\frac{x}{|x|}$ is the degree-one radial solution of the planar Ginzburg--Landau equation. This gives an affirmative answer to Open Problem~2.2 in Brezis' open-problem list. This is achieved by comparing the radial solution $f$ in the disk with the degree-one radial solution $F$ in the whole plane. Multiplying a disk competitor by $F/f$ enables us to use the known minimality of the whole-plane vortex without changing the boundary trace. The difference of the two energies can be decomposed into Fourier modes. Every nonzero mode is nonnegative, and the zero mode is then handled by a Picone type identity.

</details>

Abstract: Our purpose is to investigate the local well-posedness of the compressible Euler equations in a two-dimensional bounded corner domain with acute contact angles. This configuration describes a free surface intersecting the fixed bottom at two points, where the fluid is subject to a gravitational field and the interface between the fluid and air is influenced by capillary forces. When the contact angles are less than $π/2$, we establish a local existence theory for the solution, with dissipation effects occurring at the contact points. The main analytical challenge arises from contact point singularities, which renders previous methods for dealing with compressible free boundary problems inadequate. To overcome this, we first establish the geometric structure for the compressible Euler equations, an approach originally introduced by Shatah and Zeng \cite{Shatah2008} for incompressible fluids. Additionally, we provide a singularity analysis for the wave equations in the corner domain, which ensures the validity of calculations near the corner. Finally, based on the geometric structure and singularity analysis, we obtain a priori energy estimates. Using these estimates, we also prove the local well-posedness of the system in a geometric formulation. To our knowledge, this is the first result addressing compressible Euler equations with a free boundary that involves contact points.

</details>

Abstract: We construct pure two-bubble solutions for the energy-critical focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equation in space dimension $N = 6$. They are global in (at least) one time direction and approach a superposition of two stationary states, both centered at the origin. One of the bubbles develops at scale $1$, whereas the length scale of the other converges to $0$ at rate $e^{-|t|}$. The phases of the two bubbles form the right angle.
  Such solutions were previously constructed in dimension $N \geq 7$. The six-dimension case presents specific difficulties, as the ground state does not belong to $\dot H^{-1}$. This prevents the use of the standard method of removing linear terms in modulation equations via suitable orthogonality conditions, due to loss of coercivity of the energy functional. The main novelty of this work is the introduction of modified modulation parameters to overcome this issue; these can be viewed as an analog of a normal form transformation in the context of modulation analysis. We also establish new coercivity estimates for the linearized energy, whose positive constants depend explicitly on the choice of the orthogonality conditions.

</details>

Abstract: We study the quantum drift-diffusion, or Derrida-Lebowitz-Speer-Spohn (DLSS), equation for a nonnegative density $\varrho$ on a bounded convex domain with Neumann boundary conditions, in the square-root variable $u=\sqrt\varrho$. We show that the DLSS operator, defined and monotone on smooth strictly positive functions, admits a unique maximal monotone extension in $L^2(Ω)$, explicitly given by the minimal (defect-free) operator plus the normal cone of the positivity constraint. The generated semigroup, which contracts the Hellinger distance between the densities, thus yields a canonical solution - existing, unique, and stable for every nonnegative $L^2$ initial datum and in every space dimension - independent of any approximation scheme: it is in fact the unique contraction semigroup extending the classical evolutions that emanate from smooth, uniformly positive data. The implicit Euler scheme converges to it, and $\sqrt u\in L^2_{\rm loc}(H^2)$ along the flow. When the datum belongs to the domain of the operator, the solution is strong and satisfies the equation pointwise, with no reaction term created on the vacuum $\{u=0\}$. We characterize the trajectories in several equivalent ways - as Bénilan integral solutions and through one-sided weak formulations - prove the maximality of the operator also in the $H^2$-$H^{-2}$ duality and, in dimension $d\le3$, identify the flow with the weak solutions in the uniqueness class of Fischer. A second-order estimate of independent interest underlies the construction: on a convex domain with Neumann conditions the dissipation $\int_Ω(Δu)^2/u\,\mathrm{d} x$ is finite exactly when $\sqrt u\in H^2(Ω)$, and it then controls the full Hessian of $\sqrt u$, in every dimension.

</details>

Abstract: We study sign-changing viscosity solutions of the singularly perturbed fully nonlinear equation $$ F(D^2u_\varepsilon) =
\fracα{\varepsilon} β\left(\frac{u_\varepsilon}{\varepsilon}\right) \qquad\text{in }B_1\subset\mathbb R^n, $$ where $F$ is uniformly elliptic and $β\in C_c (-1,1)$ is nonnegative. We prove the scale-sharp estimate $$ \|\nabla u_\varepsilon\|_{L^\infty(B_{1/2})} \leq C\left( \|u_\varepsilon\|_{L^\infty(B_1)}+\sqrtα\right), $$ with $C$ depending only on the dimension, the ellipticity constants, and $β$, and independent of $\varepsilon$ and $α$. The difficulty is structural: at positive $\varepsilon$ there is neither a free boundary nor a prescribed transmission law, while the general fully nonlinear setting provides no monotonicity formula capable of controlling the interaction of the two phases. An intrinsic transition-region estimate reduces the problem to linear growth from buffered level boundaries. The resulting dyadic continuation is closed by a large-slope stopping argument: bounded accumulated slopes yield the desired growth directly, whereas unbounded slopes force the effective reaction strength to vanish after normalization and lead to a contradiction. Result: 建立了紧致黎曼流形切丛上动力学Fokker-Planck方程的全局正则化与指数收敛结果，在L²和L¹两种设定下均获得了亚椭圆正则化与亚压缩平衡化的系统性刻画，并通过插值不等式、谱间隙与熵不等式将多种已有结果简化并统一，同时提出了若干新的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为动力学Fokker-Planck方程在几何框架下的研究提供了全面而自洽的理论工具，统一了相对论动力学与几何分析的视角，简化和推广了已有成果，并为后续研究指明了新方向。

Abstract: These notes are devoted to the kinetic Fokker--Planck equation on the tangent bundle of a compact Riemannian manifold, with motivations coming from relativistic kinetic theory of gases (à la Debbasch) and geometric analysis (à la Bismut). They are developed in particular from collaboration with Fabrice Debbasch and Yann Ollivier. The presentation is mostly self-contained, intended for readers without much prior familiarity with geometric analysis. Once the kinetic equations are reviewed, I expand the basic toolbox from functional and spectral analysis. Then the main focus is on global hypoelliptic $C^\infty$ regularization and hypocoercive exponential equilibration, both of which are addressed both in $L^2$ and $L^1$ settings. Various types of interpolation inequalities, spectral gap and entropic inequalities are established and used. Several existing results are simplified and unified, along with new ones. Some directions of research are mentioned.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 本文建立了Coulomb排斥梯度流在有界密度框架下的定量平均场收敛与传播混沌理论，收敛阶由初始密度的L∞范数和时间显式刻画。同时证明了一般指数s∈(d-2,d)的Riesz弱-强稳定性与唯一性，并通过异常点构造说明归一化Wasserstein收敛与调制能量收敛之间本质上是不同的收敛概念，不可相互替代。

Abstract: We establish quantitative mean-field convergence and propagation of chaos for repulsive Coulomb gradient flows at the bounded-density regularity of the limiting equation. The argument couples the dissipative modulated-energy identity with the normalized quadratic transport cost of the full $N$-particle law. The remaining negative mean-square force-error term is used through an exact completion of squares after mollification: the non-Lipschitz remainder is absorbed by this negative term, while a sharp first-order commutator estimate is applied to the mollified Lipschitz field. For the Coulomb equation, the sharp $L^\infty$ decay gives the density envelope $m(t)=\|ρ_0\|_{L^\infty}/(1+t\|ρ_0\|_{L^\infty})$. A density-adapted transport weight and mollification scale $m(t)^{-1/d}$ yield an Osgood comparison. Thus, for every $d\ge2$ and $ρ_0\in\mathcal P_2(\mathbb R^d)\cap L^\infty(\mathbb R^d)$, we obtain quantitative comparison with the global bounded-density Coulomb solution on every prescribed finite interval. For tensorized initial data, the normalized squared Wasserstein distance of the full $N$-particle law, the expected modulated energy, and the time-integrated mean-square force error are bounded by $N^{-2γ_{T,d}/d}$ for $d\ge3$ and $((1+\log N)/N)^{γ_{T,2}}$ for $d=2$, where $γ_{T,d}=(1+T\|ρ_0\|_{L^\infty})^{-c_d}$. For $d-2<s<d$, we also prove Riesz weak--strong stability for prescribed reference solutions in $L^\infty(0,T;B^{s-d+2}_{\infty,q})$, with Gronwall, Bihari, and Osgood comparisons according to $q$, together with uniqueness in the stated Besov class. Finally, an outlier construction separates modulated-energy convergence and Kac chaos from normalized Wasserstein convergence of the full $N$-particle law.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了强竞争条件下对称两种群Lotka-Volterra竞争-扩散模型中传播速度的符号。证明了对任意强竞争强度大于1且扩散比d≠1，行波解总向扩散更快物种的方向传播扩展其领地，当d=1时速度为零，并建立了波速和行波对模型参数的平滑依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在两种群Lotka-Volterra竞争-扩散模型中，双稳行波解的传播速度符号决定了哪个物种最终入侵另一个物种，这是理解竞争排斥机制的核心问题。此前该结论（"Unity is not strength"定理）仅在受限参数区域内被证明，需要在整个参数区域内给出完整刻画。

Method: 主要方法包括：(1) 证明当扩散率不同时，不存在单调驻定前沿，这是符号刻画的关键步骤；(2) 结合波速对模型参数的连续性；(3) 利用系统的物种交换对称性；(4) 在特定参数值处构造显式行波解，通过这些工具组合，在整个参数区域内确定了传播速度的符号。

Result: 对任意强竞争强度大于1及任意扩散比d≠1，行波解使扩散更快物种的领地扩张；当d=1时传播速度恰好为零。所有参数区域内传播速度符号均已完全确定，从而建立了完整的"Unity is not strength"定理，并证明了波速和行波对参数的平滑依赖。

Conclusion: 该研究完整解决了强竞争情形下对称两种群Lotka-Volterra竞争-扩散模型中传播方向的问题，证实了扩散更快物种总是获胜，将此前仅在有限参数区域成立的"Unity is not strength"定理推广到整个参数区域，为竞争扩散系统的动力学理解提供了完备的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper provides a complete characterization of the sign of the propagation speed in the symmetric two-species Lotka-Volterra competition-diffusion model under strong competition. The system admits a unique bistable travelling front and the sign of its speed determines which of the two species invades the other. We prove that for every strong competition intensity greater than $1$ and every diffusion ratio $d\neq1$, the travelling front propagates so as to expand the territory occupied by the faster-diffusing species. The front has a zero speed exactly when $d=1$. We also establish the smooth dependence of the wave speed and the travelling front on the model parameters. The main step in the sign characterization is to prove that a monotone standing front cannot exist when the diffusion rates are different. Combined with continuity of the wave speed with respect to the parameters, the species-exchange symmetry of the system, and an explicit travelling front at a particular parameter value, we obtain the sign of the propagation speed throughout the entire parameter region. This establishes the ``Unity is not strength'' theorem, which was previously known only in restricted parameter regimes.

</details>

Result: 获得了两个主要结果：(1) 连接h_L的导数与极小化轨迹对偶弧的一阶灵敏度关系；(2) 二阶定量估计，证明h_L在局部范围内半凹、在短时间内半凸，且在特定变量下一致凸。

Conclusion: 该工作为接触背景下奇点传播与广义梯度流的分析建立了内在方法论基础，对弱KAM理论、最优输运以及粘性解正则性研究具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: This paper studies generalized Hamiltonian gradient flows for contact-type Hamilton--Jacobi equations, adopting the variational framework of Herglotz's principle and its fundamental solution \(h_L(t,x,y,u)\). Two main results are presented. First, precise first-order sensitivity relations are derived, linking derivatives of \(h_L\) to dual arcs of minimizing trajectories. Second, quantitative second-order estimates show that \(h_L\) is locally semiconcave and, over short time, semiconvex, indeed uniformly convex in certain variables. These regularity properties follow from a detailed variational analysis of minimizing trajectories. The work establishes a foundation for intrinsic methods in analyzing singularity propagation and generalized gradient flows in contact context, with implications for weak KAM theory, optimal transport, and regularity of viscosity solutions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khan的互反公式原本适用于标准设定，将其推广到短区间设定具有重要意义，因为短区间上的二次矩分析面临更多技术困难（如近似函数展开、指数和的估计等），且需要厘清扭曲参数与区间长度的依赖关系。

Method: 利用黎曼ζ函数的近似函数（approximate functional equation）、对扭曲二次矩进行解析展开，并通过互反公式（reciprocity formula）刻画短区间上的互反关系。区间长度T^δ中δ的取值范围(1/2,1)表明区间长度介于√T和T之间，需要精细处理指数和与平稳相位分析。

Result: 成功在短区间设定下证明了扭曲二次矩的互反公式，该公式显式揭示了扭曲参数与区间长度T^δ之间的依赖关系，作为Khan结果的自然推广。

Conclusion: 该结果将互反公式的适用范围从标准区间扩展到短区间，为研究黎曼ζ函数在临界线上短区间内的统计性质提供了新工具，同时为后续可能的应用（如亚凸性估计、矩猜想等）奠定了基础。

Abstract: We prove a reciprocity formula for a twisted second moment of the Riemann zeta function over a short interval of length $T^δ$, where $δ\in (\frac{1}{2},1)$, centered at height $T$ on the critical line. This result extends Khan's reciprocity formula to the short interval setting and makes explicit the relation between the twisting parameters and the interval length.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解L函数族中低零点的分布是解析数论的核心问题，特别是零点在中心点附近的聚集对算术（如BSD猜想）有重要意义。此前对偶全纯尖点新形式族中低零点数量的下界研究仍有改进空间，本文旨在给出更精确的定量刻画。

Method: 采用密度定理和矩方法。首先构造沿素数水平序列的无限序列并使用一阶矩/密度定理推导N^{7/8}logN的下界；其次计算高阶中心矩（如二阶及以上矩），选取合适的检验函数以捕捉窗口内零点的存在性，从而证明正比例；最后通过矩的Chernoff型界或类似技术获得上尾多项式界，进而得到正比例形式具有有界非零低零点的结论。

Result: (1) 沿无限素数水平序列，具有低零点的形式数量≥N^{7/8}logN；(2) 正比例（positive proportion）的族具有指定窗口内的零点；(3) 得到零点数量的多项式上尾界；(4) 正比例形式具有有界且非零的低零点数量。

Conclusion: 本文在GRH假设下显著改进并量化了低零点存在性定理，结果揭示了该新形式族中低零点的分布具有正密度且数量有界的结构性特征，为理解L函数族统计性质提供了更深入的证据。

Abstract: We study low-lying zeros in families of even holomorphic cuspidal newforms of fixed weight and prime level, with particular emphasis on the number of forms having a zero in a prescribed normalized window about the central point and on the distribution of the number of such zeros among the forms. We quantify the number of forms having at least one zero in the window and study the distribution of the number of zeros in that window among the forms. Assuming the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis, we obtain new lower bounds for the number of forms having a low-lying zero. We first prove that, along an infinite sequence of prime levels $N$, the number of such forms is $\gg N^{7/8}\log N$. We then use higher centered moments and an appropriate test function to show that a positive proportion of the family has a zero in a prescribed window. Finally, we obtain polynomial upper-tail bounds for the number of zeros occurring there and show that a positive proportion of forms have a bounded, nonzero number of low-lying zeros.

</details>

*Federico Accossato,Nadir Murru,Giuliano Romeo,Giulia Salvatori*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 得到了改进的Maillet-Baker准周期连分数判据的函数域模拟；证明了Uchiyama-Roth定理的定量版本；获得了代数元素收敛子分母增长的Davenport-Roth型上界；证明了特征零函数域中回文连分数要么是二次要么是超越的。

Conclusion: 在特征零函数域中，回文连分数满足与实数情形相同的二分法（二次或超越），但证明方法不同；定量Roth型估计为连分数超越性研究提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: Given a field $K$, let $K((T^{-1}))$ be the field of formal power series. Continued fractions in $K((T^{-1}))$ can be defined by analogy with classical real continued fractions and have been widely studied. Some results establish the transcendence of elements of $K((T^{-1}))$ arising from special families of continued fractions, but much remains to be explored. In this paper, assuming that $K$ has characteristic zero, we improve the known analogues of the Maillet--Baker criteria for quasi-periodic continued fractions. A central tool that we prove is a quantitative version of Uchiyama's analogue of Roth's theorem in function fields, which gives an explicit bound for the number of exceptionally good rational approximations to an algebraic power series. This quantitative estimate also yields a Davenport--Roth-type upper bound on the growth of the denominators of the convergents of algebraic elements. Finally, we prove that palindromic continued fractions are either quadratic or transcendental, as in the real case, but using a different proof strategy.

</details>


### [93] [Algebraic geometric framework of Rogers--Ramanujan identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15219)
*Yifeng Huang,Kenny Lau,Ken Ono,Peter Paule*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Huang-Jiang-Oblomkov关于Rogers-Ramanujan型q级数恒等式的猜想在a=3情况下的若干实例，并借助一个更精细的和-和恒等式（该恒等式后被Lau和Ono一般化证明），完整建立了a=3的情形。所有恒等式已在Lean证明助手中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rogers-Ramanujan恒等式将二次形式控制的q级数与模5的两个剩余类支撑的无穷乘积联系起来，这类恒等式非常稀少且缺乏系统构造方法。Huang、Jiang和Oblomkov提出了一种新来源：对每对互素整数(a,b)构造无穷秩q级数Z_{a,b}(q)，并猜想其等于(a-1)(b-1)/2个模a+b的模单位的乘积。此前仅有a=2（即Andrews-Gordon恒等式）被证实，a>2的任何情形都未知，这是该领域的核心空白。

Method: 作者针对(a,b)=(3,4),(3,5),(3,7),(3,8)证明该猜想。证明路径通过一个更精细的和-和恒等式（sum-to-sum identity）来完成，该恒等式对所有与3互素的b被猜想成立，并在q=1时对所有b被证明。最终该恒等式的一般情形由Lau和Ono独立证明，从而完成a=3的完整情形。所有恒等式还通过AxiomProver在Lean定理证明器中完成了形式化验证。

Result: 成功证明了猜想在(a,b)=(3,4),(3,5),(3,7),(3,8)四个具体情形下成立；证明了辅助的和-和恒等式在q=1时对所有与3互素的b成立；该恒等式被Lau和Ono推广证明后，完整解决了a=3的情形。所有相关恒等式已在Lean中形式化验证。

Conclusion: 本文为Huang-Jiang-Oblomkov关于Rogers-Ramanujan型恒等式新来源的猜想在a=3情形下提供了完整证明，填补了此前无a>2实例的空白。工作还展示了形式化验证在组合数论和q级数恒等式研究中的应用价值，并为后续a>3情形的探索奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Rogers--Ramanujan identities equate a $q$-series whose exponents are governed by a quadratic form with an infinite product supported on two residue classes modulo~$5$. Identities of this shape are scarce, and a central problem is to identify the structures that produce them in families. Huang, Jiang, and Oblomkov have proposed a source of a new kind: to each pair of coprime integers $a,b>1$ they attach an infinite-rank $q$-series $Z_{a,b}(q)$, assembled from counts of commuting nilpotent matrix pairs $(A,B)$ with $A^a=B^b$ over finite fields, and they conjecture that it equals an explicit product of $(a-1)(b-1)/2$ modular units of level $a+b$. The $a=2$ cases are the Andrews--Gordon identities; no case with $a>2$ was known. We prove the conjecture for $(a,b)=(3,4)$, $(3,5)$, $(3,7)$, and $(3,8)$. Our proofs pass through a finer sum-to-sum identity, which we conjecture for all $b$ coprime to $3$ and establish for all $b$ when $q=1$. Lau and Ono have since proved that identity in general, and with it the full $a=3$ case. These identities have been formalized and verified in Lean by AxiomProver.

</details>


### [94] [Interlacing for zeros of the Serre derivative of Eisenstein series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15248)
*Maggie Bohanek,Owen McGinty,Erick Ross,Yanhui Su,Hui Xue*

Main category: math.NT

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rankin和Swinnerton-Dyer在1970年证明了Eisenstein级数$E_k$的非椭圆零点全部位于基本域的下弧$\{e^{i\theta}:\pi/2<\theta<2\pi/3\}$上。最近Sugibayashi将该性质推广到Serre导数$\vartheta_k(E_k)$。本文的动机是进一步精确确定这些零点的具体位置，并利用这些位置估计来研究不同权重之间零点的交错关系，以及尖点投影零点的分布性质。

Method: 本文首先对Serre导数$\vartheta_k(E_k)$在下弧上的零点位置给出非常精确的估计（location estimates）。基于这些精确位置估计，推导出四组主要结果：(1) 证明对任意$\ell>k$，$\vartheta_\ell(E_\ell)$的零点与$\vartheta_k(E_k)$的零点在下弧上满足Stieltjes交错；(2) 精确分类了$\vartheta_\ell(E_\ell)$与$\vartheta_k(E_k)$零点在下弧上何时满足标准交错；(3) 证明$\vartheta_k(E_k)$的零点与$E_{k+2}$的零点在下弧上始终满足标准交错；(4) 利用第三个结果，证明$\vartheta_k(E_k)$的尖点投影的所有零点都位于下弧上，推广了Xue和Zhu的结果。

Result: 本文的四个主要结果均成功得到证明：Stieltjes交错性对所有$\ell>k$成立；标准交错的成立条件被完整分类；$\vartheta_k(E_k)$与$E_{k+2}$的标准交错性无例外成立；尖点投影的零点下弧性质得到推广。

Conclusion: 通过精确的零点位置估计，本文建立了Eisenstein级数与Serre导数之间系统性的零点交错理论，并在尖点投影方面推广了已有结果，深化了对模形式零点在基本域下弧上分布规律的理解。

Abstract: In 1970, Rankin and Swinnerton-Dyer showed that the non-elliptic zeros of Eisenstein series $E_k$ in the fundamental domain all lie on the lower arc $\{ e^{iθ}: \fracπ{2} < θ< \frac{2π}{3}\}$. Very recently, Sugibayashi showed that the same property also holds for the Serre derivative $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ of Eisenstein series. In this paper, we first give very precise estimates for where exactly these zeros are located on the lower arc. These location estimates then allow us to prove four main results. First, we show that the zeros of $\vartheta_\ell(E_\ell)$ Stieltjes interlace with the zeros of $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ on the lower arc for all $\ell > k$. Second, we classify precisely when the zeros of $\vartheta_\ell(E_\ell)$ (standard) interlace with the zeros of $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ on the lower arc. Third, we show that the zeros of $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ always (standard) interlace with the zeros of $E_{k+2}$ on the lower arc. Fourth, as an application of the third main result, we show that the zeros of the cuspidal projection of $\vartheta_k(E_k)$ all lie on the lower arc, extending a result of Xue and Zhu.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该结果在高维情况下显著改善了符号不确定性常数的估计，展示了对Fourier本征函数与拟模形式工具的有效应用。

Abstract: For every positive integer $d$ divisible by $4$, we prove the following new upper bound for the Bourgain-Clozel-Kahane sign uncertainty constant: \[
  \mathrm{A}_+(d) \le \sqrt{2 \left\lfloor \frac{d}{16} \right\rfloor + 2}. \] It recovers the optimal bound $\mathrm{A}_+(12) \le \sqrt{2}$ in dimension $12$ and improves the previously best known bound $\sqrt{(d+2)/(2π)}$ for all $d \ge 52$ divisible by $4$. The proof uses Fourier eigenfunctions and associated quasimodular forms constructed by Feigenbaum, Grabner, and Hardin.

### [96] [Singular-weight Conway-invariant Jacobi forms of index four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15431)
*Daren Dong*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明李奇格Λ的Co₀不变全纯Jacobi形式空间J^{Co₀}_{12,Λ,4}的维数恰好为6，并通过Conway对称性、T-不变性、level-4 Hecke代数给出的普适关系以及约化模2和深洞格的数据，完成了此前Sun和Wang留下的精确维数问题。文中还构造了四个已知形式之外的额外两个Conway平均theta形式，从而给出了该空间的一个自然基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sun和Wang已证明Co₀不变的奇异权12、指标4的全纯Jacobi形式空间的维数介于4和9之间，但未能确定精确值。由于该空间在奇异权下与同时Co₀与Weil不变的高散傅里叶变换空间（C[Λ/4Λ]上的不变子空间）同构，其精确维数的确定对理解Leech lattice相关的模形式理论和Conway-Symmetry具有重要理论意义。

Method: 1. 利用theta分解将问题转化为C[Λ/4Λ]上同时Co₀与Weil不变的子空间；2. 借助Conway对称性和T-不变性将问题约化到12维的各向同性轨道和空间；3. 由level-4 Hecke代数推出投影Weil S-算子满足普适关系S(S+½I)(S−I)=0，等价地，整系数特征矩阵K满足K(K+2²³I)(K−2²⁴I)=0；4. 结合已知的指标4形式、模2约化、以及A₃⁸深洞得到的特征数据缩小可能性；5. 通过对已知指标3形式Φ_{12,3}的挠性估值确定最后一个特征值，最终排除得到唯一容许分支，维数为6。

Result: 证明了dim J^{Co₀}_{12,Λ,4}=6。除Sun和Wang此前展示的四个形式外，作者利用Niemeier格（根系统D₆⁴和D₄⁶）的显式标记构造了两个Conway平均theta形式，合起来给出了该6维空间的一个自然基。

Conclusion: 该结果彻底解决了Sun和Wang留下的精确维数问题，并提供了完整的构造性基。所使用的方法——结合Hecke代数关系、模2约化和深洞数据——为研究其他奇异权Jacobi形式维数提供了一套可推广的框架。

Abstract: Let $Λ$ be the Leech lattice and let $\mathrm{Co}_0=\operatorname{Aut}(Λ)$. Sun and Wang proved that the space of $\mathrm{Co}_0$-invariant holomorphic Jacobi forms of singular weight $12$ and index $4$ satisfies \[ 4\leq \dim J^{\mathrm{Co}_0}_{12,Λ,4}\leq 9, \] and left its exact dimension open. We prove \[ \dim J^{\mathrm{Co}_0}_{12,Λ,4}=6. \] At singular weight, theta decomposition identifies this space with the simultaneous $\mathrm{Co}_0$- and Weil-invariant subspace of $\mathbb{C}[Λ/4Λ]$. Conway symmetry and $T$-invariance reduce the problem to a twelve-dimensional space of isotropic orbit sums. On this space the projected Weil $S$-operator satisfies the universal relation \[ S\left(S+\frac{1}{2}I\right)(S-I)=0, \] obtained from the level-$4$ Hecke algebra. Equivalently, the associated integral character matrix $K$ satisfies \[ K(K+2^{23}I)(K-2^{24}I)=0. \] Combining this relation with known index-$4$ forms, reduction modulo $2$, and character data obtained from the $A_3^8$ deep hole reduces the remaining possibilities to a finite exact calculation. A final torsion evaluation of the known index-$3$ form $Φ_{12,3}$ determines the last required character value, and exact elimination leaves a unique admissible branch, of dimension $6$. We also construct two Conway-averaged theta forms from explicit markings of the Niemeier lattices with root systems $D_6^4$ and $D_4^6$. Together with the four forms previously exhibited by Sun and Wang, they give a natural basis of $J^{\mathrm{Co}_0}_{12,Λ,4}$.

In even weight we construct a quotient of the formal double zeta space which surjects onto the space of finite multiple zeta values of depth at most four, and we attach to every even period polynomial an explicit relation among the values $ζ_{A}(2a,1,2b,1)$.

### [99] [Cycles of supersingular elliptic curves for pairing-based proof systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15523)
*Craig Costello,Gaurish Korpal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了新的配对友好椭圆曲线循环的构造方法，使用超奇异椭圆曲线（而非此前唯一的普通MNT循环），旨在支持无界递归配对证明系统。虽然超奇异循环定义在扩域上导致G1元素和计算效率略低，但其每个实例都可以通过Bröker算法高效构造，而MNT循环只能构造有限数量的实例。超奇异循环还允许灵活选择底层有限域，并能与其他定义在更小有限域上的配对友好曲线连接形成"棒棒糖"结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归配对证明系统需要可无界使用的配对友好椭圆曲线循环。此前唯一已知的循环是MNT循环（普通曲线），但通过CM方法只能实际构造有限数量的MNT实例，无法真正实现无限循环。此外，2019年研究者提出的"棒棒糖"结构（将循环与定义在更小有限域上的配对友好曲线连接）一直悬而未决，本文旨在解决这一激励性问题。

Method: 利用超奇异椭圆曲线构造配对友好循环。通过Bröker算法可以高效构造无限多个超奇异曲线实例，不同于MNT循环受限于CM方法的有限可行性。超奇异循环定义在二次扩域上（最优情况），允许灵活选择满足特定条件的素数p（如p-1含有大幂次的2因子）。文中还详细研究了利用超奇异循环将循环与其他定义在更小有限域上的配对友好曲线连接以构造"棒棒糖"结构。

Result: 构造了新的无限族超奇异配对友好椭圆曲线循环。虽然超奇异循环定义在二次扩域上，使得G1中的元素和计算不如MNT循环紧凑高效，但每个实例均可通过Bröker算法高效构造，从而在实践中可实现的循环数量是无限的。此外，超奇异循环使得连接其他更小有限域上的配对友好曲线成为可能，实现了"棒棒糖"结构。

Conclusion: 超奇异循环在理论上的无限性和实践中的可构造性上优于MNT循环，虽然存在一定的计算效率折衷，但为递归配对证明系统提供了更实用的曲线构造方案，并成功解决了2019年提出的"棒棒糖"连接问题。

Abstract: We give new constructions of cycles of pairing-friendly elliptic curves with a view towards unbounded recursive pairing-based proof systems. Unlike the only known prior cycle of elliptic curves - the ordinary MNT cycle - our construction uses elliptic curves that are supersingular.
  A trade-off of our approach is that the supersingular cycles are defined over extension fields (quadratic extensions in the optimal case), which makes elements and computations in $\mathbb{G}_1$ less compact and efficient than those in the MNT cycle. On the other hand, the supersingular cycles in this paper offer a key advantage over their MNT counterpart: every instance of our infinite family of supersingular curves can be efficiently constructed via Br{ö}ker's algorithm, whereas it is only feasible to construct a relatively small, bounded number of MNT instances via the CM method. In other words, while both constructions give infinite families of pairing-friendly curves in theory, only the supersingular construction gives rise to infinite numbers of cycles that can be realised in practice.
  Supersingular cycles offer benefits that are relevant in the context of recursive pairing-based proof systems. They afford flexibility in the choices of underlying finite fields; one can choose primes $p$ for which the underlying field arithmetic is efficient and for which $p-1$ is divisible by a large power of 2. Or, as we study in detail, using supersingular cycles unlocks the possibility of connecting the cycle with other pairing-friendly elliptic curves that are defined over much smaller finite fields, where proof system arithmetic is much more efficient. Indeed, constructing these so-called lollipops of pairing-friendly curves was the motivating problem (posed by researchers back in 2019) that inspired the present work.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond endoscopy for $\mathsf{GL}_2$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ with ramification 5: cancellation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15553)
*Yuhao Cheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完成了Langlands提出的Beyond Endoscopy框架下，Q上GL_2在ramified（分歧）情形下的工作。证明了在任意光滑测试函数（S={∞,q_1,...,q_r}且2∈S）下，标准表示的迹公式各项在n<X求和时均为o(X)，并直接证明了GL_2/Q的"迹公式极限形式"恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Langlands提出的Beyond Endoscopy计划旨在通过分析迹公式的渐近行为来证明函子性猜想。此前的GL_2在非分歧（unramified）情形下的工作已经完成，本文旨在将该结果推广到分歧（ramified）情形，即允许任意光滑测试函数而不限于特定的局部测试函数，从而完成GL_2/Q在分歧情形的全部工作。

Method: 作者利用Arthur关于加权轨道积分傅里叶变换的结果，重写涉及交错算子（intertwining operators）的项；然后将展开式分别与实情形（Arthur-Herb-Sally和Hoffmann的结果）以及非阿基米德情形（直接利用Arthur定义进行精确计算）的结果进行比较，从而证明迹公式各项的渐近估计为o(X)，并直接证明带变量X的"迹公式极限形式"恒等式。

Result: 证明了在包含有限素数q_1,...,q_r且2∈S的局部齐次化集合S={∞,q_1,...,q_r}上取任意光滑测试函数时，标准表示的迹公式各项在X→∞下求和均为o(X)；并直接建立了GL_2/Q的带变量X的迹公式极限形式恒等式。

Conclusion: 本文填补了Beyond Endoscopy框架下GL_2/Q在分歧情形（允许任意光滑测试函数）下的最后空白，完成了该问题的全部工作，为后续将其推广到更高秩群或其他自守形式场景奠定了基础。

Abstract: We complete our work on $\mathsf{GL}_2$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ in the ramified setting for \emph{Beyond Endoscopy} proposed by Langlands. We prove that the asymptotic formula for each term of the trace formula when summing over $n<X$ with arbitrary smooth test functions at places in $S=\{\infty,q_1,\dots q_r\}$ with $2\in S$, for the standard representation, is $o(X)$. We prove an identity with a variable $X$, called the \emph{limit form of the trace formula} for $\mathsf{GL}_2$ over $\mathbb{Q}$, directly. The proof uses Arthur's result on the Fourier transform of weighted orbital integrals to rewrite the term involving intertwining operators, and then compares the expansion with the results of the real case due to Arthur-Herb-Sally and Hoffmann, and the nonarchimedean case by direct computation using Arthur's definition.

</details>


### [101] [The principal series 2-representation for $\mathrm{GL}_n\times\mathrm{GL}_n$ over a 2-dimensional local field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15623)
*Xuecai Ma,Jingwen Zhu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在二重Borel子群上通过有序元组α定义交叉K₂乘子，经扭曲等变化构造出H的精确一致光滑范畴主级数2-表示；对于酉参数，利用R((X))测度在球面部分上给出正定的形式Hermite内积，为选定的非分歧结构提供了二维范畴类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在更高维范畴化框架下推广非分歧的表示论结构，将经典的主级数表示、Hermite内积等概念提升到2-范畴层面，为Langlands纲领的分类似物提供范畴化基础。

Method: 首先在F=F_q((u))((t))的二重Borel子群上定义交叉K₂乘子（由有序元组α=(α₁,…,αₙ)参数化），然后通过扭曲等变化构造GL_n(F)_L×GL_n(F)_R的精确一致光滑范畴主级数2-表示。对于酉参数情形，利用R((X))测度在球面部分上赋予Hom值形式Hermite内积。

Result: 成功构造了H的精确一致光滑范畴主级数2-表示，并在酉参数条件下建立了球面部分上Hom值的正定形式Hermite内积。

Conclusion: 本文给出的构造为选定的非分歧结构提供了二维范畴类比，证明了经典的表示论构造可以在2-范畴层面系统性地推广。

Abstract: Let $F=\mathbb{F}_q((u))((t))$ and $H=\mathrm{GL}_n(F)_L\times\mathrm{GL}_n(F)_R$. An ordered tuple $α=(α_1,\dots, α_n) $ defines a cross-$K_2$ multiplier on the doubled Borel. Twisted equivariantization then yields an exact uniformly smooth categorical principal series $2$-representation of $H$. For unitary parameters, the $\mathbb R((X))$-measure
  gives a positive Hom-valued formal Hermitian inner product on the spherical part. These constructions provide two-dimensional categorical analogues of selected unramified structures.

</details>


### [102] [An exact formula for Erdős' problem 1005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15681)
*Yanmohan Wang,Mingxu Xie,Ziyuan Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了 Erdős 问题 1005 的完整解决方案：通过解析方法证明了 f(n) 在 n 足够大时的精确公式，并结合有限计算机验证，确定了所有 n≥4 的整数 f(n) 的精确值。这解决了 Erdős 于 1943 年提出的关于 Farey 序列中分子分母反向排序项之间最小项数的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős 在 1943 年提出的问题涉及 Farey 序列中相邻分数之间分子分母反向排列的最小项数 f(n)，其中确定常数 c 使得 f(n)=(c+o(1))n 被称为 Erdős 问题 1005。虽然 Cipollini 最近证明了 c=1/4 的渐近结果，但精确公式尚未被完全确定。

Method: 本文在 Cipollini 的框架基础上，采用解析证明方法推导出 f(n) 对所有足够大的 n 的精确公式。然后结合有限的计算机验证，进一步将所有 n≥4 的整数值的 f(n) 完全确定。

Result: 成功推导出 f(n) 对足够大的 n 的精确公式，并通过计算机验证覆盖了所有剩余的有限情形，最终确定了对每个整数 n≥4 的 f(n) 精确值。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了 Erdős 问题 1005，不仅确认了渐近常数为 1/4，而且给出了所有 n≥4 情况下 f(n) 的精确值，将解析方法与计算机验证相结合，为该经典数论问题画上了句号。

Abstract: In 1943, Erdős considered the minimum number $f(n)$ of terms between two fractions in the Farey sequence of order $n$ whose numerators and denominators are oppositely ordered. Determining the constant $c$ in $f(n)=(c+o(1))n$ is known as Erdős Problem 1005. Recently, Cipollini solved this asymptotic problem by proving that $f(n)=(1/4+o(1))n$. Following his framework, we give an analytic proof of an exact formula for $f(n)$ for all sufficiently large $n$. Combining this with a finite computer verification, we further determine $f(n)$ for every integer $n\geq 4$.

</details>


### [103] [Hausdorff dimension of $τ$-approximable points on self-similar sets in $\mathbb R^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15686)
*Yubin He,Lingmin Liao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了自相似集与丢番图逼近的交集的维数问题。对于满足开集条件的强不可约迭代函数系统生成的非单点自相似集K，证明了存在一个阈值ε_K，使得当维数参数τ在区间(1/d, 1/d+ε_K)内时，K与W_d(τ)（即具有q^{-τ}逼近阶的丢番图逼近点集）的交集具有无穷大的Hausdorff s(τ)-测度，从而确定了其Hausdorff维数。在一维情形，该结果验证了Bugeaud-Durand关于中间三分Cantor集的猜想公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立自相似集与有理点逼近问题的深层联系。丢番图逼近与分形几何的交叉问题是近年来的热点，将经典丢番图逼近理论（有理点对任意点的逼近）推广到自相似集上的限制逼近问题，并验证Bugeaud-Durand猜想。

Method: 利用满足开集条件(OSC)的强不可约迭代函数系统生成自相似集的结构，结合Mass Transference Principle类技术和渐近几何测度论方法，构造合适的覆盖与测度估计，证明Hausdorff测度的无穷性与维数公式。

Result: 证明了对于任意非单点自相似集K（由满足OSC的强不可约IFS生成），存在ε_K>0使得当1/d<τ<1/d+ε_K时，Hausdorff s(τ)-测度H^{s(τ)}(K∩W_d(τ))=∞，其中s(τ)=δ+(d+1)/(1+τ)-d，从而K∩W_d(τ)的Hausdorff维数恰为该公式。在一维中间三分Cantor集情形，该结果在τ>1且充分接近1时确认了Bugeaud-Durand猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作将自相似集的丢番图逼近维数理论推广到了高维情形，并在临界值附近完全解决了维数公式，特别验证了Bugeaud-Durand猜想在τ接近1时的正确性，为更一般的丢番图逼近与分形几何交叉问题提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Let $d\geq 1$. Let $K\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ be a non-singleton self-similar set generated by a finite strongly irreducible iterated function system satisfying the open set condition, and let $δ=\dim_{\mathrm H} K$. For $τ>1/d$, set \[ W_d(τ) = \left\{ \mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^d: |q\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{p}|<q^{-τ} \text{ for infinitely many }(\mathbf{p},q)\in\mathbb{Z}^d\times\mathbb{N} \right\}. \] We prove that there exists $\varepsilon_K>0$ such that, for every $1/d<τ<1/d+\varepsilon_K$, \[ \mathcal{H}^{s(τ)}(K\cap W_d(τ))=\infty, \qquad\text{with } s(τ):=δ+\frac{d+1}{1+τ}-d, \] and consequently \[ \dim_{\mathrm H}(K\cap W_d(τ)) = δ+\frac{d+1}{1+τ}-d. \] In dimension one, specializing to the middle-third Cantor set, this establishes the Bugeaud--Durand conjectural formula for $τ>1$ sufficiently close to $1$.

</details>


### [104] [Large values of quadratic character sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15773)
*Zikang Dong,Ruihua Wang,Weijia Wang,Hao Zhang,Shengbo Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了二次Dirichlet特征和的极大值问题，在广义黎曼猜想（GRH）假设下，证明了短和长二次特征和的新的Omega结果，改进了以往的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次Dirichlet特征和是解析数论中的核心研究对象，其极大值（Omega结果）刻画了特征和的下界增长行为。在GRH假设下推导更精确的Omega结果，有助于深入理解二次特征和的分布规律。

Method: 在GRH假设下，利用二次Dirichlet特征与类数的联系以及黎曼zeta函数在临界线附近的性质，通过解析数论中的标准技巧（如特征和的矩估计、零密度估计等）来构造新的Omega下界。

Result: 获得了短和长二次Dirichlet特征和的新Omega结果，优于此前已知的结论。

Conclusion: 在GRH假设下，二次Dirichlet特征和的极大值下界可以被进一步改进，为理解二次特征和的内在结构提供了更精确的刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate large values of quadratic Dirichlet character sums. We prove new Omega results for both short and long quadratic character sums under the assumption of the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis (GRH), which improve the previous results.

</details>


### [105] [Better than square-root cancellation in Piatetski-Shapiro sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15807)
*Renjie Zhu,Tianping Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明当A为Piatetski-Shapiro序列时，Steinhaus或Rademacher随机乘性函数f(n)在该序列上的和展现出优于平方根抵消现象。同时，受Harper(2023)工作启发，证明了Piatetski-Shapiro序列上特征和的典型大小为o(√|N_c(x)|)，进而对几乎所有模素数p的特征，特征和可达到Weil界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继Harper(2019)在自然数集上证明优于平方根抵消现象，Xu(2023)在R-rough数上、Hardy和Xu(2026)在y-smooth数上推广后，本文旨在验证当A为Piatetski-Shapiro序列时该现象是否依然存在，即A不满足乘法能量尽可能小（(2+o(1))|A|²）的条件时，该结论是否仍成立。

Method: 将Piatetski-Shapiro序列A作为求和集合，对Steinhaus和Rademacher随机乘性函数f(n)在A上的和进行分析；同时借鉴Harper(2023)的技术，研究Piatetski-Shapiro序列上的特征和典型大小，并利用此结果论证对几乎所有模素数p的特征，特征和可达Weil界。

Result: 证明了在Piatetski-Shapiro序列上，优于平方根抵消现象依然存在，提供了另一个正面的例子（尽管该集合不具备最小乘法能量性质）。此外，证明了Piatetski-Shapiro序列上特征和的典型大小为o(√|N_c(x)|)，由此可推出对几乎所有模素数p的特征，特征和达到Weil上界。

Conclusion: 该结果为优于平方根抵消现象提供了更广泛的适用场景，表明该现象并不局限于具有最小乘法能量的集合；同时，对Piatetski-Shapiro序列上特征和的典型大小给出了精确刻画，并建立其与经典Weil界的联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate whether the better than square-root cancellation phenomenon exists for $\sum^{}_{n\leq X,n\in \mathcal{A}}f(n)$ when $\mathcal{A}$ is a Piatetski-Shapiro sequence and $f(n)$ is a Steinhaus or Rademacher random multiplicative function. Harper's remarkable breakthrough (2019) showed that better than square-root cancellation phenomenon happens when $\mathcal{A}$ takes natural integers set $\mathbb{N}$. Then Max Wenqiang Xu (2023) proved the conclusion also holds if $\mathcal{A}$ consists of $\mathcal{R}$-rough numbers. The similar result can be obtained for $y$-smooth numbers according to Hardy and Xu's recent paper(2026). Our result provides another positive example about the existence of better than square-root cancellation phenomenon when $\mathcal{A}$ is not a set with multiplicative energy as small as $(2+o(1))|\mathcal{A}|^2$. Furthermore, inspired by Harper's work (2023), we also prove the typical size of character sums over Piatetski-Shapiro sequences is $o(\sqrt{|\mathcal{N}_c(x)|})$. Based on this, the character sums over Piatetski-Shapiro sequences can reach Weil's bound for almost all characters modulo a prime $p$.

</details>


### [106] [Weighted singular vectors in common-base self-similar sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15845)
*Taehyeong Kim,Jaemin Park*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了满足开集条件的仿射张成、同基数整自相似集中加权完全无理奇异向量的Hausdorff维数下界，并针对中三分康托正方形改进了此前已知的显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异向量在丢番图逼近和动力系统中具有重要地位，而加权完全无理奇异向量在自相似集中的维数下界问题此前仅有较弱的估计。作者希望给出更精确的一般性下界，并为中三分康托正方形这一经典分形集合改进已有的维数估计。

Method: 论文通过利用仿射张成、同基数整自相似集的结构性质以及开集条件，结合Hausdorff维数的估计技术（如质量分布原理或覆盖论证），证明了一个关于加权完全无理奇异向量的维数下界定理；对中三分康托正方形的特殊情况进行了专门分析以获得改进的bound。

Result: 得到了满足开集条件的仿射张成同基数整自相似集中加权完全无理奇异向量集的Hausdorff维数下界，且该下界在中三分康托正方形情形下优于此前已知的显式下界。

Conclusion: 该结果推广并改进了已有文献中关于奇异向量在自相似分形集中维数的下界，为加权奇异向量与分形几何交叉领域提供了新的定量结论。

Abstract: We prove a lower bound for the Hausdorff dimension of weighted totally irrational singular vectors in affine-spanning common-base integral self-similar sets satisfying the open set condition. For the middle-third Cantor square, the bound improves the previously known explicit lower bound.

</details>


### [107] [On the residual Eisenstein cohomology of unitary groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15947)
*Harald Grobner*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意幺正群$U(V)$在二次扩张$E/F$上的剩余Eisenstein上同调，聚焦于极大抛物子群的贡献，识别了Eisenstein级数的上同调相关极点，并证明了所得残差在显式次数上作为非平凡的自守上同调类存在。通过研究$F=\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt[3]{2})$上$F$-秩为3的具体幺正群实例，显式构造了满足主定理假设的尖点自守表示，从而显式构造了非零剩余Eisenstein上同调类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望系统理解任意幺正群$U(V)$的剩余Eisenstein上同调结构，特别是极大抛物子群贡献的极点与残差类，并希望通过具体实例验证和展示所涉及的现象范围，为更一般的自守上同调理论提供方法和证据。

Method: 利用Eisenstein级数的解析性质来识别上同调相关的极点，结合自守上同调理论分析残差类的非平凡性。在具体例子中，通过基变换（base change）方法在$F=\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt[3]{2})$上显式构造满足主定理假设的尖点自守表示，进而构造非零的剩余Eisenstein上同调类。

Result: 对任意幺正群$U(V)$的极大抛物子群贡献，识别了Eisenstein级数上同调相关的极点，证明所有残差在显式给定的次数上作为非平凡自守上同调类存活。在$F$-秩为3的具体幺正群案例中，成功显式构造了满足主定理条件的尖点自守表示及非零剩余Eisenstein上同调类。

Conclusion: 本文建立的剩余Eisenstein上同调理论适用于任意幺正群，且显式构造的方法具有范式意义，可通过基变换推广到其他地面域$F$上的幺正群，为自守上同调的深入研究提供了系统框架和可操作的方法。

Abstract: We investigate the residual Eisenstein cohomology of an arbitrary unitary group $U(V)$ attached to an arbitrary quadratic extension of number fields $E/F$. Our focus lies on the contribution of the maximal parabolic $F$-subgroups of $U(V)$, for which we identify the cohomologically relevant poles of Eisenstein series and prove that the resulting residues all survive as non-trivial classes in automorphic cohomology in an explicit degree. To illustrate the range of phenomena involved, we study in detail the case of a unitary group over $F=\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt[3]{2})$ of $F$-rank $3$, for which we explicitly construct cuspidal automorphic representations, which satisfy all the assumptions of our main theorem and hence explicitly construct non-zero residual Eisenstein cohomology classes for this unitary group. The methods used in this construction are paradigmatic however, i.e., generalize to other unitary groups over other ground fields $F$ by the use of base change.

</details>


### [108] [Simple and distinct zeros in a prime-modulus Dirichlet family at mesoscopic shrinking height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16034)
*Zhixu Hua,Xiufan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在无广义黎曼假设（GRH）的前提下，无条件地证明了当奇素数q趋向无穷时，对q的非主特征进行无权重平均后，ζ函数与q相关的L函数在区间I=(Q^{-α},2Q^{-α})内非平凡零点数量的渐近公式，以及简单零点和判别零点在总零点中的比例下界，比例常数分别为C_MT≈0.6725和C_d≈0.8363。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究L函数在临界线附近（物理高度趋于零）的零点分布密度和下界比例，是在不依赖任何未证明假设（如广义黎曼假设）的情况下，深入理解零点统计结构和局部平均间距行为的核心问题。以往结果多依赖强假设，本文希望给出无条件的结果。

Method: 证明结合了三种技术：(1) 小高度计数与零密度消去法（small-height counting and zero-density deletion）；(2) Weil的Hermitian形式的有限光滑Gabor压缩（finite smooth Gabor compression）；(3) 矩阵的一阶、二阶矩结合基于惯性（inertia）的秩-迹不等式。通过这些工具推导出三个计数界的下界。

Result: 证明了三个主要结果：零点总数的精确渐近公式N_q = (q(log q)^{1-α})/(2π)(1+o_α(1))；简单零点和临界线判别零点的比例下极限均至少为C_MT≈0.6725；全部判别零点的比例下极限至少为C_d≈0.8363。物理高度趋于零而区间内含约(log q)^{1-α}个局部平均间距。

Conclusion: 本文在完全无条件的框架下给出了L函数在极小高度处零点计数与比例下界的确定性结果，不依赖广义黎曼假设。所获常数C_MT与C_d具有显式精确值，为该方向的研究提供了新的硬性下界。

Abstract: Fix $0<α<1$. Let $q$ tend to infinity through odd primes, put $Q=\log q$, and set $I=(Q^{-α},2Q^{-α}]$. Averaging without weights over the $q-2$ nonprincipal characters modulo $q$, let $\mathcal N_q$ be the number of nontrivial zeros in $I$, counted with multiplicity; let $\mathcal N^s_{0,q}$ and $\mathcal N^*_{0,q}$ count, respectively, simple and distinct critical-line zero points; and let $\mathcal N_{d,q}$ count all distinct zero points, without a location restriction. We prove unconditionally that \[ \mathcal N_q=\frac{q(\log q)^{1-α}}{2π}\{1+o_α(1)\}, \] \[ \liminf_{q\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal N^s_{0,q}}{\mathcal N_q} \ge C_{\mathrm{MT}},\qquad \liminf_{q\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal N^*_{0,q}}{\mathcal N_q} \ge C_{\mathrm{MT}},\qquad \liminf_{q\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal N_{d,q}}{\mathcal N_q} \ge C_d, \] where \[ C_{\mathrm{MT}}=\frac32-\frac1{\sqrt2}\cot\left(\frac1{\sqrt2}\right) =0.672500703679\ldots, \qquad C_d=\frac{1+C_{\mathrm{MT}}}{2}=0.836250351839\ldots . \] The physical height tends to zero, whereas $I$ contains $\asymp(\log q)^{1-α}$ local mean spacings. The proof combines small-height counting and zero-density deletion with a finite smooth Gabor compression of Weil's Hermitian form. First and second matrix moments, together with an inertia-based rank--trace inequality, yield the three counting bounds. No form of the generalized Riemann hypothesis is assumed.

</details>


### [109] [Superspecial Points on Shimura Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16036)
*Yasuhiro Terakado,Jiangwei Xue,Chia-Fu Yu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了不定四元数Q-代数B及其极大序O_B所对应的Shimura曲线在任意素数p模约化后的超特殊位点，给出了超特殊F_q-有理点存在的显式判据，并通过Eichler类数公式和Selberg迹公式计算了几何超特殊点数和F_p-有理超特殊点数，推广了Deuring关于超奇异椭圆曲线的显式公式及Ibukiyama-Katsura公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shimura曲线的模p约化及其超特殊位点结构是算术几何中的核心问题，但目前缺少对任意素数p下超特殊点存在性与计数的一般性理论，需要将已有的超奇异椭圆曲线和超特殊abelian曲面结果推广到Shimura曲线的情形。

Method: 通过推广Ribet对秩2容许四元数双模的分类（舍弃容许性假设），完整分类了超特殊O_B-abelian曲面所附着的Dieudonné模；在此基础上利用Eichler类数公式计算几何超特殊点数，利用Selberg迹公式计算F_p-有理超特殊点数，从而得到超特殊F_q-有理点存在的显式判据。

Result: 给出了超特殊F_q-有理点存在的显式判据；计算出了几何超特殊点数与F_p-有理超特殊点数的精确公式；完成了超特殊O_B-abelian曲面的Dieudonné模分类，推广了Ribet的分类结果；所得公式推广了Deuring的超奇异椭圆曲线公式，并给出了Ibukiyama-Katsura公式的Shimura曲线类比。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套完整的Shimura曲线超特殊位点理论，统一并推广了超奇异椭圆曲线与超特殊abelian曲面两个经典情形的显式公式，为Shimura曲线算术提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Let $X$ be the Shimura curve attached to an indefinite quaternion $\mathbb{Q}$-algebra $B$ with a maximal order $O_B$. This paper investigates the reduction $X\otimes \mathbb{F}_p$ of $X$ modulo an arbitrary prime $p$, focusing particularly on its superspecial locus. We give an explicit criterion for the existence of superspecial $\mathbb{F}_q$-rational points on $X$. Furthermore, we compute both the number of geometric superspecial points and the number of $\mathbb{F}_p$-rational superspecial points, through the Eichler class number formula and the Selberg trace formula. As a key ingredient, we classify the Dieudonné modules attached to superspecial $O_B$-abelian surfaces, which generalizes Ribet's classification of admissible quaternion bimodules of rank $2$ by dropping the admissible hypothesis. These results generalize Deuring's explicit formula for supersingular elliptic curves over $\mathbb{F}_p$ and give the Shimura-curve analogue of the Ibukiyama-Katsura formulas for principally polarized superspecial abelian surfaces over $\mathbb{F}_p$.

</details>


### [110] [A dynamical generalization of Chowla's conjecture on average](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16108)
*Biao Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Bergelson和Richter关于素数定理的动力系统推广工作的启发下，证明了Chowla猜想沿平均意义下的动力系统推广形式，并进一步将其推广到沿素数序列的平均版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chowla猜想是数论中的经典难题，描述Liouville函数在平移参数下取值渐近无关。作者希望借助动力系统方法（Bergelson和Richter的工作）来获得该猜想在平均意义下的动力系统推广，从而为攻克原猜想提供新视角。

Method: 作者遵循Qi和Zheng的方法，该方法在不可约二元三次形式上建立了Bergelson和Richter定理的变体，并以此作为证明动力量Chowla猜想平均形式的核心工具。同一方法还用于证明沿素数序列的动力量Chowla猜想平均形式。

Result: 成功证明了Chowla猜想在动力系统框架下沿平均意义的推广，以及沿素数序列的相应平均版本。

Conclusion: 该工作将Chowla猜想与动力系统理论相结合，通过Bergelson-Richter方法及Qi-Zheng的变体，建立了更一般化的平均性结果，丰富了Liouville函数相关猜想的研究工具和视角。

Abstract: Let $k\ge1$ be an integer and let $λ$ be the Liouville function. In 1965, Chowla gave a conjecture that the values of $λ(n+h_1),\dots, λ(n+h_k)$ are asymptotically unrelated for any distinct natural numbers $h_1, \dots, h_k$. In this article, motivated by the recent work of Bergelson and Richter on the dynamical generalizations of the prime number theorem, we will show a dynamical generalization of Chowla's conjecture on average. In the proof, we follow an approach of Qi and Zheng who established a variant of Bergelson and Richter's theorem over irreducible binary cubic forms. Moreover, we will use this approach to show an analogue of the dynamical Chowla's conjecture along the primes on average as well.

</details>


### [111] [The multiplication table problem in large dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16163)
*Cihan Sabuncu,Christian Táfula*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了k维乘法表问题M_k(N)，即在{1,...,N}中取k个数相乘能得到的不同乘积数量。利用Khovanskii定理，对于足够大的k，M_k(N)是k的π(N)次多项式。本文确定了其首项系数的渐近大小，并推广到将因子限制为y-光滑整数的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法表问题（即乘积集合的基数问题）是数论与组合学中的经典问题。Khovanskii定理虽然保证了M_k(N)对于足够大的k为k的多项式，但多项式的具体系数（特别是首项系数）的渐近大小此前未知。确定这一系数的大小对于深入理解乘法表的渐近结构至关重要。

Method: 本文使用Khovanskii定理结合解析数论中的素数分布方法，对N→∞且k相对于N足够大时，M_k(N)的首项系数进行渐近分析，得到约为exp((2π+o(1))√N/log N)的渐近形式。同时，对y-光滑整数情形（y=o(log N)），利用光滑数的分布性质证明不同乘积数量与N^k以内y-光滑整数数量渐近相等，且对k≥1一致成立。

Result: (1) 当N→∞且k足够大（相对于N）时，M_k(N) = exp((2π+o(1))√N/log N)·k^{π(N)}/π(N)!，确定了首项系数的渐近大小。(2) 对y=o(log N)的情形，证明将因子限制为y-光滑整数时，k个此类整数乘积的不同数量与N^k以内y-光滑整数的数量渐近相等，且对k≥1一致成立。

Conclusion: 本文完整确定了k维乘法表问题中首项系数的渐近阶，深化了Khovanskii定理在该问题上的应用；同时给出了y-光滑因子情形的完整刻画，为乘法表问题的进一步研究提供了新的工具和结果。

Abstract: For $N\geq 2$ and $k\geq 1$, let $M_k(N):=\#\{x_1\cdots x_k : x_i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}\text{ for all } i\}$ be the $k$-dimensional multiplication table. Given $N$, Khovanskii's theorem implies that $M_k(N)$ agrees, for all sufficiently large $k$, with a polynomial in $k$ of degree $π(N)$. We determine the asymptotic size of its leading coefficient, proving that, as $N\to\infty$, with $k$ sufficiently large relative to $N$, \[ M_k(N) = \exp\bigg((2π+o(1))\frac{\sqrt{N}}{\log N}\bigg)\frac{k^{π(N)}}{π(N)!}. \] We also study the analogous problem when the factors are restricted to $y$-smooth integers. For $y=o(\log N)$, we prove that the number of distinct products of $k$ such integers up to $N$ is asymptotic to the number of $y$-smooth integers up to $N^k$, uniformly for $k\geq 1$.

</details>


### [112] [Oort's conjecture on supersingular abelian varieties in odd characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16405)
*Valentijn Karemaker,Chia-Fu Yu*
