We also establish stability and removal results for families of extremal size satisfying $e(\mathcal F)<2^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor-1}$, showing that such a family is close to an extremal eventown family and can be made eventown by deleting a small number of its members.

</details>


### [158] [The matching extendability of optimal 2-planar graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16408)
*Xinyao Li,Heping Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了最优2-平面图的匹配可扩展性。证明了任意偶阶4连通最优2-平面图是1-可扩展的，并给出了其2-可扩展的判定准则；证明了不存在5-可扩展的最优2-平面图，并基于十二面体构造了一个4-可扩展的最优2-平面图；最后证明了任意偶阶6连通且顶点数至少为2m+2的最优2-平面图是距离3的m-可扩展图（对任意m≥0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-平面图是平面图概念的推广，其中每条边最多被两条其他边交叉。最优2-平面图（边数达到上界5|V|-10）具有重要的极值图论意义。匹配可扩展性是图论中研究图的结构鲁棒性的重要概念，目前对最优2-平面图的匹配可扩展性研究尚属空白，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用局部最优性（local optimality）作为核心分析工具，结合连通性、阶数奇偶性等图论技术，对最优2-平面图的匹配可扩展性进行系统研究。通过构造性方法（基于十二面体构造4-可扩展图）和反证/判定准则等手段证明各类可扩展性结果。

Result: (1) 所有偶阶4连通最优2-平面图均为1-可扩展的；(2) 给出了这类图2-可扩展的充要条件（判定准则）；(3) 不存在5-可扩展的最优2-平面图，但存在4-可扩展的最优2-平面图（基于十二面体构造）；(4) 每个偶阶6连通、顶点数≥2m+2的最优2-平面图是距离3的m-可扩展图（任意m≥0）。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了最优2-平面图的匹配可扩展性层级：在4连通偶阶条件下可保证1-和2-可扩展，在6连通条件下可进一步保证距离3的m-可扩展性；同时确定了匹配可扩展度的上界（不存在5-可扩展图），并验证了该上界是紧的（存在4-可扩展图）。

Abstract: A graph is 2-planar if it can be drawn in the plane such that each edge is crossed by at most two other edges. It is known that for a 2-planar graph $G$, $|E(G)| \le 5|V(G)| - 10$. When the equality holds, we call $G$ an optimal 2-planar graph. This paper investigates the matching extendability of optimal 2-planar graphs. By local optimality, we prove that every 4-connected optimal 2-planar graph $G$ of even order is 1-extendable, and give a criterion for $G$ to be 2-extendable. We also prove that no optimal 2-planar graph is 5-extendable and construct a 4-extendable optimal 2-planar graph based on the dodecahedron. Finally, we show that every 6-connected optimal 2-planar graph of even order with at least $2m+2$ vertices is distance 3 $m$-extendable for any $m \ge 0$.

</details>


### [159] [A new flag-transitive linear space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16412)
*Seyed Hassan Alavi,Ashraf Daneshkhah,Martin W. Liebeck,Cheryl E. Praeger*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个新的旗传递2-(496,4,1)设计，其自同构群为PΓL₂(32)，纠正了有限旗传递线性空间分类中的一个遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限旗传递线性空间的分类中遗漏了一个设计，需要构造并纠正这个分类错误。

Method: 构造一个新的旗传递2-(496,4,1)设计，以PΓL₂(32)作为其自同构群。

Result: 成功构造了一个新的旗传递2-(496,4,1)设计，其自同构群为PΓL₂(32)，填补了分类中的遗漏。

Conclusion: 该构造纠正了有限旗传递线性空间分类中的一个遗漏，完善了已有分类结果。

Abstract: We construct a new flag-transitive $2$-$(496,4,1)$ design with automorphism group $\mathrm{PΓL}_2(32)$. This corrects an omission in the classification of the finite flag-transitive linear spaces.

</details>


### [160] [On the finite group whose proper enhanced power graph is claw-free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16434)
*Sudip Bera,Andrea Lucchini*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限群G的真增强幂图E**(G)为无爪图（claw-free）的条件。首先证明E(G)无爪当且仅当G为循环群；当E**(G)无爪时，给出G/cyc(G)的结构刻画：可解非幂零群中G为亚循环群或G/cyc(G)为Frobenius群或2-Frobenius群；不可解群中G/cyc(G)同构于PSL(2,q)或PGL(2,q)，并据此完成非可解群的真增强幂图为无爪图的完全分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强幂图是群论与图论交叉领域中重要的研究对象，而无爪图是图论中具有丰富结构性质的一类图。作者旨在通过对真增强幂图无爪性的研究，进一步理解有限群的结构，特别是利用循环化子(cyclicizer)这一工具来刻画满足该性质的群。

Method: 作者首先证明E(G)无爪当且仅当G为循环群。利用E(G)的支配顶点构成的循环子群——循环化子cyc(G)，通过分析E**(G)的无爪性对商群G/cyc(G)的结构进行精细刻画，分别讨论可解非幂零情形与不可解情形，并利用PSL(2,q)和PGL(2,q)等线性群的分类结果完成非可解群的完全分类。

Result: (1) E(G)无爪当且仅当G是循环群；(2) 若G可解但非幂零且E**(G)无爪，则G为亚循环群，或G/cyc(G)为Frobenius群或2-Frobenius群；(3) 若G非可解且E**(G)无爪，则G/cyc(G)同构于PSL(2,q)或PGL(2,q)；(4) 给出了非可解群的真增强幂图为无爪群的完全分类。

Conclusion: 本文通过"无爪性"这一图论性质，结合循环化子等群论工具，完整刻画了真增强幂图为无爪图的有限群的结构，特别是在非可解情形下给出了精确到线性群同构的完全分类，深化了对增强幂图与有限群结构之间联系的认知。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group. The \emph{enhanced power graph} of $G$, denoted by $\mathcal{E}(G)$, is the graph with vertex set $G$ in which two vertices $u$ and $v$ are adjacent if and only if there exists an element $w \in G$ such that both $u$ and $v$ belong to $\langle w \rangle$. The \emph{proper enhanced power graph} of $G$, denoted by $\mathcal{E}^{**}(G)$, is the subgraph of $\mathcal{E}(G)$ induced by the non-dominating vertices.
  The main objective of this paper is to investigate finite groups whose proper enhanced power graph is claw-free, that is, contains no induced subgraph isomorphic to the complete bipartite graph $K_{1,3}$. We first prove that $\mathcal{E}(G)$ is claw-free if and only if $G$ is cyclic. The set of dominating vertices of $\mathcal{E}(G)$ forms a cyclic subgroup of the center of $G$, namely the \emph{cyclicizer} $\cyc(G)$ of $G$. This allows us to give a precise description of the structure of $G/\cyc(G)$ when $\mathcal{E}^{**}(G)$ is claw-free. If $G$ is solvable but not nilpotent, then $G$ is metacyclic, or $G/\cyc(G)$ is either a Frobenius group or a $2$-Frobenius group. If $G$ is non-solvable, then $G/\cyc(G)$ is isomorphic to $\PSL(2,q)$ or $\PGL(2,q),$ and this allows us to give a complete classification of the non-solvable groups whose proper enhanced power graph is claw-free.

</details>


### [161] [Lattice balls with large additive energy in discrete cubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16444)
*Xinyu Long*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究高维球内整数格点的加法能量（additive energy）问题，给出了一个关于维度d一致的估计，将Z^d ∩ B_d(R)的归一化加法能量与连续球B_d(R)的能量进行比较。当R_d/√d→∞时，归一化能量的d次方根收敛于4√3/9。作为应用，构造了A_n集合，其加法能量的对数满足特定渐近公式，回答了Shao的问题。还证明了在Gram矩阵坐标下，连续球能量的指数率由正则四面体的Gram矩阵确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加法能量是加性组合学和数论中的基本量，衡量一个集合中相等的二元和的数量。高维球内整数格点的加法能量在高维极限下的行为此前未被充分理解，尤其是在维度d趋于无穷时的一致估计。此外，Shao提出了一个关于满足某些能量条件的集合构造问题，需要新的显式构造方法来解决。

Method: 本文通过将离散格点的加法能量与连续球的能量建立联系，利用高维几何和积分估计技术获得一致于d的估计。在连续能量方面，使用Gram矩阵坐标转换，将问题归结为一个固定维度上的行列式最大化问题，并证明其极值点为正则四面体的Gram矩阵。应用部分则通过构造特定的集合A_n并利用前述能量估计推导其渐近行为。

Result: 主要结果为：当R_d/√d→∞时，Z^d∩B_d(R_d)的归一化加法能量的d次方根收敛到4√3/9（约0.7698）。由此构造的集合A_n满足log E(A_n)=3log|A_n|-d log(3√3/4)+o(d)。取d=⌊(log n)^{1/2}⌋时，给出了Shao所提问题的显式构造答案。此外证明连续球能量的指数率由正则四面体的Gram矩阵（固定维度的行列式最大化问题）所决定。

Conclusion: 本文建立了高维离散加法能量与连续能量之间的精确指数渐近关系，发现极限常数4√3/9由正则四面体的Gram矩阵决定这一几何本质。该结果不仅提供了统一的维度一致估计，还通过显式构造回答了Shao的问题，展示了高维加性组合学与经典凸几何（正则四面体）之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: For a finite set $A$ in an abelian group, let \[
  E(A)=\#\{(a_1,a_2,a_3,a_4)\in A^4:a_1+a_2=a_3+a_4\}. \]
  We obtain an estimate uniform in $d$ that compares the normalized additive energy of $\mathbb{Z}^d \cap B_d(R)$ with the continuous energy of $B_d(R)$ .
  If $R_d/\sqrt d\to\infty$, then \[
  \lim_{d\to\infty}
  \left(
  \frac{E\bigl(\mathbb{Z}^d\cap B_d(R_d)\bigr)}
  {\lvert \mathbb{Z}^d\cap B_d(R_d)\rvert^3}
  \right)^{1/d}
  =\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{9}. \] As an application, consider \[
  A_n
  =
  R_n\mathbf{1}_d
  +
  \bigl(\mathbb{Z}^d\cap B_d(R_n)\bigr), \] where $d=d(n)\to\infty$ satisfy $\log d=o(\log n)$, and $R_n=\lfloor(n-1)/2\rfloor$. Then $A_n\subset\{0,1,\ldots,n-1\}^d$ and \[
  \log E(A_n)
  =3\log|A_n|-d\log\frac{3\sqrt3}{4}+o(d). \] In particular, taking $d=\lfloor(\log n)^{1/2}\rfloor$ gives an explicit construction answering a question of Shao \cite{Shao2026}. We also prove that in Gram-matrix coordinates, the exponential rate of the continuous ball energy is determined by a fixed dimensional determinant maximization whose extremizer is the Gram matrix of a regular tetrahedron.

</details>


### [162] [Measurable Matroids: Foundations and Min--Max Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16464)
*Kristóf Bérczi,András Imolay,Ádám Schweitzer*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在标准无原子测度空间上发展了拟阵理论的测度论类比，建立了可测拟阵的独立集、基、秩函数和闭包算子的等价描述，并证明了可测版本的Edmonds拟阵交定理、拟阵并定理以及Nash-Williams–Tutte定理，应用于二部图化中的可测匹配、容量约束运输和超有限生成森林的覆盖与打包问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到子模集函数的商收敛框架启发，作者希望在有限拟阵理论的极限侧识别合适的可测对象，建立其基础结构与优化理论，从而将经典拟阵理论推广到连续（测度）情形。

Method: 采用测度论方法在标准无原子测度空间上定义可测拟阵，通过独立集、基、秩函数和闭包算子四种等价描述刻画之。建立了可测拟阵的截断、伸长、直和、对偶和子式等操作，证明了可测版本的Edmonds拟阵交定理和拟阵并定理（含公共基达成定理）。在二分图化上利用划分拟阵得到Hall亏缺公式和连续b匹配的最小最大定理，并通过秩扩张判据保证超限收敛的达成性，最终推导出图化的可测Nash-Williams–Tutte定理。

Result: 证明了可测拟阵的多种等价刻画；建立了完整的可测拟阵结构理论；获得可测Edmonds交定理（以超限代替最大）和并定理的秩公式；导出二部图化的精确Hall亏缺公式和连续b匹配min-max定理；给出秩扩张判据下超限达成条件，并推广Lyons–Nazarov可测完美匹配定理；证明图化的可测Nash-Williams–Tutte定理，刻画超有限本质生成森林的近似覆盖/打包与精确分解。

Conclusion: 本文为有限拟阵理论建立了完整的测度论类比，证明了可测拟阵在结构与优化层面均保持经典理论的支柱性结论（交、并、对偶、子式、匹配定理等），并将这些结果成功应用于图化与连续匹配等分析场景，表明可测拟阵是有限拟阵理论在极限侧的恰当对象。

Abstract: We develop a measure-theoretic analogue of matroid theory on standard atomless measure spaces. Motivated by the quotient-convergence framework for submodular set functions, our aim is to identify suitable measurable objects on the limit side of finite matroid theory and to develop their basic structural and optimization theory. We prove that measurable matroids admit equivalent descriptions by independent sets, bases, rank functions, and closure operators. The class includes normalized finite matroids, cycle matroids of graphings, and measurable analogues of partition, nested, lattice path, transversal, and matching matroids. We establish measurable analogues of truncation, elongation, direct sum, duality, and minors.
  We prove measurable versions of Edmonds' matroid intersection theorem and the Edmonds--Fulkerson matroid union theorem, together with an attainment theorem for common bases. The intersection theorem retains the classical min--max form, with the maximum replaced in general by a supremum. Applied to partition matroids, it gives an exact Hall-deficiency formula for measurable matchings in bipartite graphings and a min--max theorem for continuous bipartite $b$-matchings, with applications to capacity-constrained transport and prescribed cross-sections. We give a rank-expansion criterion under which the supremum is attained. For bipartite graphings, this criterion specializes to the measurable perfect matching theorem of Lyons and Nazarov.
  For measurable union, the Edmonds--Fulkerson rank formula remains valid. As a main application of measurable union and its attainment theorem, we prove measurable Nash-Williams--Tutte theorems for graphings, characterizing approximate coverings and packings by hyperfinite essential spanning forests and obtaining exact decompositions under strengthened rank inequalities.

</details>


### [163] [A Family of Simultaneously Cospectral Trees for Degree-Distance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16497)
*Limeng Lin,Quanyu Tang,Kehua Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文通过构造无限族非同构树对，反驳了Alfaro和Zapata关于所有树由Ddegp和Ddeg谱决定的猜想。每对树在17r-15个顶点上，同时关于A、L、Q、D、Ddegp、Ddeg六个矩阵是同谱的。同时给出一个符号翻转观察，表明对二分图，Atrs和Atrsp相似且具有相同的Smith标准型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的各种矩阵的谱刻画是谱图理论的核心问题。Alfaro和Zapata基于最多20个顶点的树的计算证据提出了所有树由Ddegp及Ddeg谱确定的猜想。作者旨在检验并反驳这些猜想，从而深化对图矩阵谱刻画的理解。

Method: 作者通过r正则化叶扩展构造了无限族树对（每对17r-15个顶点），并利用等划分约简（equitable-partition reduction）技术，使得这些树对同时关于A、L、Q、D、Ddegp、Ddeg六个矩阵同谱。此外，作者还利用对角±1矩阵的相似变换证明了二分图的Atrs与Atrsp相似且Smith标准型相同。

Result: 构造了无限族非同构树对，每个满足r≥3，顶点数为17r-15，同时关于六个矩阵A, L, Q, D, Ddegp, Ddeg同谱，从而反驳了原猜想。同时证明了二分图中Atrs与Atrsp通过正负1对角矩阵相似，具有相同的Smith标准型，因此对树而言两者的谱问题和Smith标准型问题是等价的。

Conclusion: 该文否定了Alfaro和Zapata提出的关于Ddegp和Ddeg谱确定树的两个猜想，并给出了连接传输邻接矩阵谱性质的重要观察，表明对二分图（特别是树）而言Atrs与Atrsp的谱理论问题可以相互转化。

Abstract: Spectral characterization of graphs for various graph matrices constitutes a central topic in spectral graph theory. Let $G$ be a graph with adjacency matrix $A(G)$, diagonal degree matrix $\Deg(G)$, distance matrix $D(G)$, and transmission matrix \(\Trs(G)\), respectively. Recently, Alfaro and Zapata (2024) introduced the degree-distance matrices \(\Ddegp(G)=\Deg(G)+D(G)\) and \(\Ddeg(G)=\Deg(G)-D(G)\), together with the transmission-adjacency matrices \(\Atrsp(G)=\Trs(G)+A(G)\) and \(\Atrs(G)=\Trs(G)-A(G)\). Based on computational evidence for trees on at most \(20\) vertices, they conjectured that all trees are determined by the spectra of \(\Ddegp\) as well as \(\Ddeg\).
  In this paper, we disprove these conjectures by constructing an infinite family of pairs of non-isomorphic trees. More precisely, for each integer \(r\ge 3\), we construct a pair of trees on \(17r-15\) vertices which are simultaneously cospectral with respect to the following six matrices \[
  A,\quad L,\quad Q,\quad D,\quad \Ddegp,\quad \Ddeg . \] The construction is based on an \(r\)-regularized leaf extension and an equitable-partition reduction. We also record a simple sign-switching observation for transmission-adjacency matrices: if \(G\) is bipartite, then \(\Atrs(G)\) and \(\Atrsp(G)\) are similar via a diagonal \(\{\pm1\}\)-matrix and have the same Smith normal form. Consequently, for trees, the spectral and Smith normal form problems for \(\Atrs\) and \(\Atrsp\) are equivalent.

</details>


### [164] [Three Infinite Families Separating Schur Positivity, the Strongly Nice Property, and the Nice Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16613)
*Kai Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了三个无穷图族，用以区分"Schur正性"、"强nice"和"nice"这三个性质：F_t（t≥6）连通且强nice但非Schur正；M_t=K_{3,3}-e ⊔ K_t（t≥3）不连通且强nice但非Schur正；N_r=K_r∨(K_2⊔2K_1)（r≥2）连通且nice但非强nice。同时证明强nice对称函数在乘法下封闭，并引入level-k nice性质且证明N_r的level深度为4r!。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图的Schur正性与图论性质之间建立联系：X_G的Schur正性蕴含G是强nice的，而强nice蕴含nice。此前人们可能认为这些性质是等价的或层级关系更紧密，本文旨在通过构造反例族说明这三个性质之间存在严格的层次分离，填补该领域的空白。

Method: 通过三个构造：1）构造连通图族F_t（t≥6）证明强nice但不Schur正；2）证明齐次强nice对称函数（具有非负单项式系数）在乘法下封闭，从而强nice图对不相交并封闭，利用此构造M_t=H⊔K_t（H=K_{3,3}-e，t≥3）作为不连通的强nice但非Schur正族；3）构造N_r=K_r∨(K_2⊔2K_1)（r≥2）证明其连通且nice但非强nice。此外引入level-k nice性质并计算N_r的level深度。

Result: 成功构造了三个无穷族分别分离三个性质的不同层级：F_t和M_t分离"强nice"与"Schur正性"，N_r分离"nice"与"强nice"。证明了强nice对称函数在乘法下的封闭性并由此得到强nice图对不相交并的封闭性，还确定了N_r的level深度为4r!。

Conclusion: Schur正性、强nice与nice这三个性质在图的层级中严格递增地弱化，且每一层之间都存在无穷多个反例。强nice性质具有良好的代数封闭性（乘法），这为后续研究提供了工具。level-depth的概念为进一步刻画nice性质的深度层级提供了新方向。

Abstract: For a graph $G$, Schur positivity of $X_G$ implies that $G$ is strongly nice, and every strongly nice graph is nice. We construct three infinite families separating these properties. We first give a connected family $F_t$, $t\ge6$, that is strongly nice but not Schur positive. We then prove that homogeneous strongly nice symmetric functions with nonnegative monomial coefficients are closed under multiplication, and hence that strongly nice graphs are closed under disjoint union. As an application, for $H=K_{3,3}-e$, the graphs \[ M_t=H\sqcup K_t,\qquad t\ge3, \] form a disconnected family that is strongly nice but not Schur positive. Finally, we define \[ N_r=K_r\vee(K_2\sqcup2K_1),\qquad r\ge2, \] and prove that every $N_r$ is connected and nice but not strongly nice. We also introduce the level-$k$ nice property and show that the level depth of $N_r$ is $4r!$.

</details>


### [165] [The maximum number of maximal dissociation sets in trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16639)
*Meiqin Wang,Min Xu,Ning Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了树的极大解离集（maximal dissociation set）的计数问题。论文刻画了任意n阶树中极大解离集的最大数量，给出了精确的分段公式g(n)，并刻画了达到该上界的极值树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yannakakis于1981年提出了解离集的概念，它是顶点集的一个子集，使得诱导子图中每个顶点的度至多为1。极大解离集在组合优化和图论中具有重要研究价值。Wang等人于2025年给出了树木极大解离集数量的上界3^{(n-1)/3}+(n-1)/3（对n≥4的树），并提出了一般情况下的猜想。本文旨在证明该猜想，给出更精确的n阶树极大解离集最大值的完整刻画。

Method: 本文通过数学归纳法及图的结构分析来证明。针对模3的不同余类（n≡0,1,2 mod 3）分别处理，逐类给出极大解离集数量的精确上界，并通过构造极值树来证明上界可达。对于特殊情形（n=9和n=21）单独处理，给出对应的极值数量19和1349。

Result: 证明了n阶树中极大解离集的最大数量为分段函数g(n)：当n=3,4,5,6时为n；当n≡1 (mod 3)且n≥7时为3^{(n-1)/3}+(n-1)/3；当n≡2 (mod 3)且n≥8时为4·3^{(n-5)/3}+n-5；当n≡0 (mod 3)且n≥12且n≠21时为16·3^{(n-9)/3}+3n-25；n=9时为19；n=21时为1349。并刻画了所有达到该上界的极值树。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Wang等人提出的关于树中极大解离集最大数量的猜想，给出了所有阶数n≥3的精确上界及对应的极值树刻画，推广并完善了已有的结论。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simple graph. A dissociation set of $G$ proposed by Yannakakis in $1981$ is defined as a set of vertices that induces a subgraph in which every vertex has a degree of at most $1$. A dissociation set is maximal if it is not contained as a proper subset in any other dissociation set. In $2025$, Wang et al.\cite{ZiyuanWang} established that for any tree $T$ of order $n\geq 4$, the number of maximal dissociation sets in $T$ is at most $3^{\frac{n-1}{3}}+\frac{n-1}{3}$ and characterized the extremal trees attaining the upper bound. They also proposed a conjecture about the upper bound of the maximal dissociation set. In this paper, we consider this conjecture and show that the maximum number of maximal dissociation sets in a tree of order $n(n\geq 3)$ is $g(n)$, where \[ g(n) = \begin{cases} n, & n=3,4,5,6,\\ 3^{\frac{n-1}{3}}+\frac{n-1}{3}, & n \equiv 1 \pmod{3},~n\geq7,\\ 4\cdot 3^{\frac{n-5}{3}}+n-5, & n \equiv 2 \pmod{3},~n\geq8, \\ 16\cdot 3^{\frac{n-9}{3}}+3n-25, & n \equiv 0 \pmod{3},~n\geq12~\text{and }~n\neq21, \\ 19, & n=9, \\ 1349, & n=21. \end{cases} \] We also characterize the extremal trees with the maximum number of maximal dissociation sets.

</details>


### [166] [Nearly permanental cospectral graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16676)
*Weifang Lv,Quanyu Tang,Wei Wang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图的近共谱（nearly cospectral）问题。Lv等人此前引入了关于行列式（特征多项式）的近共谱图问题，并给出了模4情形下的部分结果。本文证明这些结果对永久（permanental多项式）同样成立，并将结果推广到所有不可约特征标对应的immanantal多项式，建立了F_2[x]中近immanantal共谱图的类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近共谱图问题是对经典共谱图问题的一种推广，由Lv等人以行列式情形引入。由于行列式和永久分别是S_n对称群中不可约特征标(1^n)和(n)对应的immanant，自然的问题是对永久情形（以及更一般的immanant情形）建立类似结论，以统一和推广此前的部分结果。

Method: 本文采用代数组合的方法。首先针对永久（permanental多项式）情形，类比Lv等人的行列式分析框架，证明其近共谱结果在模4条件下成立。随后利用immanant的一般定义，将结论进一步推广到所有不可约特征标χ_λ对应的immanantal多项式，并在F_2[x]（二元域上的多项式环）中建立近immanantal共谱图的类似结果。

Result: 本文成功证明了Lv等人在行列式情形下的近共谱图结果对永久情形同样成立；进一步地，对所有不可约特征标对应的immanantal多项式，在F_2[x]中建立了近immanantal共谱图的类似结论，从而将行列式和永久这两种特殊情形统一到更一般的immanant框架下。

Conclusion: 本文系统地推广了近共谱图问题的研究范围，从行列式（特征多项式）扩展到永久（permanental多项式）及所有immanantal多项式情形，在模4和F_2[x]条件下给出了一致的结论，为近共谱图理论提供了更广泛的理论基础。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simple graph of order $n$ with adjacency matrix $A= (a_{ij})$. The \emph{determinant} and the \emph{permanen}t of the matrix $A$ are defined as \[\mathrm{det}A= \sum_{σ\in S_n}\mathrm{sgn}(σ) \prod_{i=1}^n a_{iσ(i)}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathrm{per}A= \sum_{σ\in S_n} \prod_{i=1}^n a_{iσ(i)},\]respectively. The polynomials $φ(G;x) =\mathrm{det}(xI-A(G))$ and $π(G;x) =\mathrm{per}(xI-A(G))$ are called the \emph{characteristic polynomial} and the \emph{permanental polynomial} of $G$, respectively. Two graphs are said to be \emph{nearly cospectral} with respect to the determinant (resp. permanent) if the difference of their characteristic (resp. permanental) polynomials is a constant. Lv et al. introduced the nearly cospectral graphs problem with respect to the determinant, and provided partial results in the case modulo 4. In this paper, we mainly prove that the corresponding results also hold for the nearly cospectral graphs problem with respect to the permanent. The determinant and permanent are the immanants corresponding to the irreducible characters $(1^n)$ and $(n)$ of the symmetric group $ S_n $, respectively. Here, the \emph{immanant} $d_λ(A)$ of $A$ is defined as \[d_λ(A) = \sum_{σ\in S_n} χ_λ(σ) \prod_{i=1}^n a_{iσ(i)},\] where $χ_λ$ is the irreducible character of $ S_n $ indexed by the partition $ λ$. The immanantal polynomial of $G$ associated with $ χ_λ$ is given by $ φ_λ(G;x)=d_λ(xI-A) $. In this paper, we also establish a similar result for nearly immanantal cospectral graphs in $\mathbb{F}_2[x]$ for all irreducible characters $χ_λ$.

</details>


### [167] [The resonance graphs of coronoid systems and nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16687)
*Lingmei Liang,Heping Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The resonance graph of a hexagonal system is connected, which shows that a perfect matching can be transformed into any other perfect matchings by a series of flips along hexagons. However, the resonance graph of a coronoid system (with holes) is not necessarily connected. Saldanha et al. (Discrete Comput. Geom. 14 (1995) 207-233) used homology and cohomology theory to obtain three versions of criteria for two tilings of a quadriculated region in the plane to be in the same connected component of the flip graph. Inspiblack by the combinatorial version, in this paper we use a purely graph-theoretical approach to give a criterion in terms of simple invariant\textcolor{black}{---flow} across cuts between holes/exterior face for two perfect matchings of a coronoid system $G$ to be in the same connected component of its resonance graph. As a corollary we obtain a criterion for the resonance graph of a coronoid system to be connected. We also discuss whether such \textcolor{black}{criteria} are applicable to nanotubes, and construct a nanotube whose resonance graph is connected, which disproves a conjecture proposed by Tratnik et al. (MATCH Commun. Math. Comput. Chem. 74 (2015) 175-186).

</details>


### [168] [Codegree Thresholds for $λ$-Choosability of Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16740)
*Chunqiu Fang,Rongxing Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图在λ-列表染色框架下的不可染色性问题。对于一个划分λ（将色集合分为q个大小分别为k_i的集合），定义λ-choosability。文章引入最小q-码度δ_q(G)作为关键参数，证明当δ_q(G)足够大时图必然不是λ-choosable，并给出了阈值函数f(λ)的上下界。对均匀划分{k,...,k}（q个相等部分），精确确定了渐近阈值ρ_q^{-(1+o(1))k}。当q=1时，恢复了Alon定理中choice number≥(1-o(1))log₂δ(G)的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu引入的λ-分配（λ-assignment）概念统一了普通着色和列表着色。Alon定理给出最小度d的图选择数至少为(1/2-o(1))log₂d，Saxton和Thomason利用超图容器方法将其改进为最佳常数1。自然的问题是：对固定的划分λ是否也有类似现象？但仅用最小度不够（平衡完全二分图的最小度任意大却始终是{1,1}-choosable的），因此需要寻找合适的替代参数。

Method: 作者引入最小q-码度δ_q(G)=min{|N_G(S)|:S⊆V(G),|S|=q}作为合适的替代参数。对于每个划分λ，证明存在整数d使得δ_q(G)≥d的图一定不是λ-choosable，记为f(λ)为最小的这样的d。采用超图容器方法（hypergraph container method）等工具证明上界f(λ)≤2^{(2q+o(1))|λ|}，并给出下界f(λ)≥(q+1)^{-1}(1+1/q)^{|λ|}。对均匀划分{k,...,k}，利用ρ_q（满足x=(1-x)^q的唯一解）确定精确渐近阈值ρ_q^{-(1+o(1))k}。

Result: (1) 对每个固定q，当|λ|→∞时，f(λ)≤2^{(2q+o(1))|λ|}；对每个λ，f(λ)≥(q+1)^{-1}(1+1/q)^{|λ|}。(2) 对q个相等部分的划分{k,...,k}，f({k,...,k})=ρ_q^{-(1+o(1))k}，其中ρ_q是x=(1-x)^q的唯一根。(3) 当q=1时，推出ch(G)≥(1-o(1))log₂δ(G)，恢复了Alon定理的渐近最优形式。

Conclusion: 最小q-码度是度量图λ-不可染色性的正确参数，它取代了最小度在此框架中的角色。文章给出了f(λ)的一般上下界，并解决了均匀划分情形的渐近精确阈值。这不仅统一了普通着色与列表着色的经典结果（q=1时恢复Alon-Saxton-Thomason定理），也首次系统回答了Zhu提出的框架下的自然推广问题。

Abstract: Let $λ=\{k_1,\ldots,k_q\}$ be a partition, and let $|λ|=k_1+\cdots+k_q$. A $|λ|$-list assignment $L$ of a graph $G$ is a $λ$-assignment if its color set can be partitioned into $q$ disjoint sets $X_1,\ldots,X_q$ such that $|L(v)\cap X_i|=k_i$ for every vertex $v$ and every $i\in[q]$. This notion, introduced by Zhu [J. Combin. Theory Ser. B, 2020], puts ordinary coloring and list coloring in the same framework. A theorem of Alon [Random Structures Algorithms, 2000] states that every graph with minimum degree $d$ has choice number at least $(1/2-o(1))\log_2d$. Saxton and Thomason [Invent. Math., 2015] later used the hypergraph container method to replace $1/2$ by the sharp constant $1$. It is natural to ask whether a similar phenomenon holds for every fixed partition $λ$. Minimum degree alone is not sufficient: balanced complete bipartite graphs have arbitrarily large minimum degree but are always $\{1,1\}$-choosable. We show that the appropriate replacement is the minimum $q$-codegree, defined for $|V(G)|\geq q$ by $δ_q(G)=\min\{|N_G(S)|:S\subseteq V(G),\,|S|=q\}$.
  More precisely, for every partition $λ$ there exists an integer $d$ such that every graph $G$ with $δ_q(G)\geq d$ is not $λ$-choosable. Let $f(λ)$ be the least such $d$. For every fixed $q$, we prove $f(λ)\leq2^{(2q+o(1))|λ|}$ as $|λ|\to\infty$, while $f(λ)\geq(q+1)^{-1}(1+1/q)^{|λ|}$ for every $λ$. For the partition $\{k,\ldots,k\}$ with $q$ equal parts, we determine the threshold asymptotically: $f(\{k,\ldots,k\})=ρ_q^{-(1+o(1))k}$ as $k\to\infty$, where $ρ_q$ is the unique $x\in(0,1)$ satisfying $x=(1-x)^q$. When $q=1$, our result implies $\operatorname{ch}(G)\geq(1-o(1))\log_2δ(G)$.

</details>


### [169] [Explicit characterization of $\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux for $n\le 4$ via $1$-$0$-slack recording tableaux in the quantum Littlewood-Richardson rule and other bijections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16800)
*Olga Azenhas*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次在量子Littlewood-Richardson规则中，利用1-0-slack记录tableaux产生的$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux，通过promotion算子复合定义Naito-Suzuki-Watanabe双射，显式地用线性不等式刻画了$n\le 4$情形下的这些tableaux，并将其与Littlewood-Richardson-Sundaram tableaux的自然双射联系起来，从而与Naito-Sagaki猜想相关的其他双射建立关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前作者已经用线性不等式刻画了量子LR规则中由1-0-slack记录tableaux产生的$\mathfrak{k}$-最高权tableaux。本文的目标是将这一刻画推广到对偶侧，即显式刻画$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux，从而更完整地理解Naito-Suzuki-Watanabe双射及Naito-Sagaki猜想的结构。

Method: 利用promotion算子的复合来定义Naito-Suzuki-Watanabe双射，将$\mathfrak{k}$-最高权tableaux与$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux对应起来；然后对$n\le 4$的情形，用线性不等式显式刻画由1-0-slack记录tableaux产生的$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux。最后通过量子LR规则中记录tableaux与Littlewood-Richardson-Sundaram tableaux的自然双射，将所得结果与其他已知双射建立联系。

Result: 对$n\le 4$的情形，成功用线性不等式显式刻画了量子LR规则中由1-0-slack记录tableaux产生的$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux；并证明了这些结果与Littlewood-Richardson-Sundaram tableaux及其他Naito-Sagaki猜想相关双射之间的相容性。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对Naito-Suzuki-Watanabe双射两侧（$\mathfrak{k}$-最高权侧和$\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant侧）的统一线性不等式刻画，并通过记录tableaux的自然双射将这些刻画与更广泛的组合双射框架联系起来，为深入理解Naito-Sagaki猜想提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Previously we have explicitly characterized by certain linear inequalities the ${\mathfrak{k}}$-highest weight tableaux in the quantum Littlewood-Richardson (LR) rule produced by $1$-$0$-slack recording tableaux. Using the composition of promotion operators to defining the Naito-Suzuki-Watanabe bijection between ${\mathfrak{k}}$-highest weight tableaux and $\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux, we now explicitly characterize by certain linear inequalities the $\widehat{\mathfrak{g}}$-dominant tableaux for $n\le 4$ produced by $1$-$0$-slack recording tableaux in the quantum Littlewood-Richardson rule. Since recording tableaux in the quantum Littlewood-Richardson rule are in natural bijection with Littlewood-Richardson-Sundaram tableaux, we relate our results with other bijections for the Naito-Sagaki conjecture.

</details>


### [170] [A Local Central Limit Theorem for Clique Counts in Sparse Random Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16882)
*Asaf Cohen Antonir,Ilay Hoshen,Maksim Zhukovskii*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意固定阶数 r≥3 的完全图 K_r，在随机图 G_{n,p} 中其副本数 X_H 满足局部中心极限定理（LCLT），限定条件为 n^{-1/m(H)} ≪ p ≤ 1/2，本质上解决了 Gilmer 和 Kopparty 关于团（clique）的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gilmer 和 Kopparty 猜想：当 H 连通、p 远大于 n^{-1/m(H)} 且 n^2(1-p) ≫ 1 时，固定图 H 在随机图中的副本数满足局部中心极限定理。此前 Berkowitz、Sah 和 Sawhney 仅对常数 p 的情形给出了证明，而对 p=o(1) 的区间，文献中仅处理了 H=K_3 的情况（Araújo 和 Mattos 在 p∈(4n^{-1/2},1/2) 中确认）。本文旨在将这一结果推广到任意团的场景。

Method: 本文采用概率方法和组合分析技术，将三角形情形下的证明框架推广到一般完全图 K_r（r≥3），在 p 的范围为 n^{-1/m(K_r)} ≪ p ≤ 1/2 时建立局部中心极限定理。方法综合了先前 Berkowitz-Sah-Sawhney 以及 Araújo-Mattos 的工作，针对团结构进行推广分析。

Result: 证明了对于任意固定 r≥3 的完全图 K_r，在随机图 G_{n,p} 中其副本数 X_H 在 n^{-1/m(H)} ≪ p ≤ 1/2 的框架下满足局部中心极限定理，从而本质上解决了 Gilmer 和 Kopparty 关于团的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文成功将 LCLT 的验证从三角形推广到任意完全图 K_r，涵盖了 p=o(1) 的稀疏区域，为 Gilmer-Kopparty 猜想的完全解决迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Let $X_H$ denote the number of copies of a fixed graph $H$ in $G_{n, p}$. Gilmer and Kopparty conjectured that $X_H$ satisfies a local central limit theorem (LCLT) provided that $H$ is connected, $p \gg n^{-1/m(H)}$, and $n^2 (1-p) \gg 1$, where $m(H)$ is the maximum density.
  Following the work of Berkowitz, Sah and Sawhney confirmed this conjecture for every constant $p$, leaving the regime where $p=o(1)$ open. In this regime, the only case addressed in the literature is when $H=K_3$, where, in a recent paper, Araújo and Mattos confirmed the conjecture for $p \in (4n^{-1/2}, 1/2)$. This, together with a general result of Röllin and Ross, essentially settles the conjecture for the triangle. We generalise these results by showing that an LCLT holds for $H = K_r$ (for any fixed $r \ge 3$) in the regime $n^{-1/m(H)}\ll p\leq 1/2$, essentially settling the conjecture for cliques.

</details>


### [171] [Cubes in the Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16883)
*Douglas Barnes,Sean Jaffe*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了用{0,1,...,p-1}^n立方体的平移覆盖n维环面(Z/qZ)^n所需的最小数量T(n,q,p)。针对p=2的情形，证明了对于每个q，存在常数1≤Λ_q≤2，使得T(n,q,2)=(Λ_q+o(1))(q/2)^n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖问题在组合数学、编码理论和离散几何中具有基础性意义。此前对T(n,q,p)的精确渐近行为了解有限，尤其是在p=2的特殊情形下，需要确定其覆盖数的渐近常数。

Method: 论文通过构造上下界来证明渐近公式：下界基于立方体覆盖的体积/计数论证，上界通过构造有效的覆盖方案或利用已知的覆盖设计工具，最终确定渐近常数Λ_q的存在性及其范围1≤Λ_q≤2。

Result: 证明了对于每个固定的q，T(n,q,2)=(Λ_q+o(1))(q/2)^n，其中Λ_q为仅依赖于q的常数，且满足1≤Λ_q≤2。

Conclusion: 给出了p=2情形下立方体平移覆盖环面问题的精确渐近刻画，将问题归结为确定常数Λ_q的具体值，为后续研究提供了理论基础和更精确的估计方向。

Abstract: For $q> p$, let $T(n,q,p)$ be the minimum number of translates of the cube \(\{0,1,\dots,p-1\}^n\) required to cover the $n$-dimensional torus $(\mathbb{Z}/q\mathbb{Z})^n$. We show that for each $q$ there exists a constant $1\le Λ_q \le 2$ such that $T(n,q,2)=(Λ_q + o(1))(q/2)^n$.

</details>
